## §3 THE ENVELOPE — HOW MANY, AND HOW GOOD (the owner's first and eighth words; items 2 and 19)

### 3.1 The four gates (each an existing read, named; every number DRAFT, UNMEASURED, FP-46 roster)

| Gate | The read | What it sets |
|---|---|---|
| TIER → the CEILING | `settlement.tier` on `SIZE_LADDER` | the most patrols and the best single band the town can ever field, whatever its wages: `thorp` 0 · `hamlet` 1 (`thin`) · `village` 2 (`thin`) · `town` 4 (`modest`) · `city` 8 (`modest`) · `metropolis` 12 (`capable`) — a metropolis keeps a standing watch whose detachments reach `strong` or `formidable` only by grouping |
| POPULATION → the POOL | the people ledger's ACTUAL census count, `settlement.population` (`peopleLedger.js :: POOLS` 'census'), against its tier's REFERENCE population — the midpoint of the tier's `POPULATION_RANGES` band, `(min + max) / 2` | `pool = max(min(1, ceiling), round(ceiling × clamp01(population / tierMidpoint)))` — a SMALL TOWN OF ITS TIER fields fewer than a full one (EXECUTED `cure/D-PR-r2-probe/pool-r2.out`: binds in 7 of 24 tier×population rows — a 901-soul town 1 of 4, a 5,001-soul city 3 of 8, a 25,001-soul metropolis 5 of 12; a hamlet keeps its one) |
| PROSPERITY → the PAY | THE BAND, always on: the settlement's PROSPERITY-DERIVED band from the landed ladder, through ST's ONE band law exported SETTLEMENT-keyed (`settlementFundingBand`, ST §2.4 — THE ONE settlement band, which ST mints and names, ST's settlement arm — `prosperityRank01 < 0.2` `unfunded` · `< 0.45` `meagre` · `< 0.8` `funded` · else `lavish`, ST §2.4; ST exports it); the DM's `wages` overlay is a CEILING over it — EFFECTIVE = min(the DM's band, the purse's band) (ST's one band law for sites and riders), so a town whose prosperity falls lets its riders thin before W-COIN lights. THE COIN is separate and a slot: when W-COIN lights, the treasury's `UPKEEP_LEDGERS` gain a `patrols` row beside `sites` and `retainers` (the W-COIN inbox; its leaf-first car named there) — so the owner's eighth word ("gated by … prosperity") stays live whatever the coin's state | `PAY_SHARE`: `unfunded` 0.25 · `meagre` 0.5 · `funded` 0.85 · `lavish` 1.0 of the pool; and the BAND: `unfunded` one band below the ceiling's, `lavish` the ceiling's |
| INTERNAL SECURITY → the WILL | `defenseLedger(settlement).internal` (the town's security investment; 0..100), floored by the live `security01Of` (a lawless town cannot claim its generated order); ONE named lean moves it — a magistrate's order (CH's `order` gift, OD-CH-50; §10.5 below), through CH's seam word `patrol_will`: `min(1, WILL_SHARE × mult)` in the weeks the magistrate's exertion stands, never past 1.0 and never a band step (a held keep moves no will: its own road rows stand lit or dark, so a will step from the same keep would count the keep twice on the roads; the owner's "increase the internal secruity of said related terriotry" is ST's own rows — ×0.9 "The keep's garrison keeps order on the roads.", ST §3.3 — never PR's). THE RESCUER'S EXERT MOVES NO WILL: an `order` paragon's exertion that answers a `captive_held` source (THE OPPORTUNITY's call — the help call at the venue, or the villain's `harm` twin there) is THE RESCUE CARD ONLY — no calling stay, no seam lean, and `patrol_will` reads NONE from it ("PR's `patrol_will` reads none", OP §14 row 37); only a magistrate's ordinary calling exertion moves `WILL_SHARE` here (the seam word `patrol_will`, CH §2.2, is unchanged; the carve-out is CH's, CH §4.5 and §6.1) | `WILL_SHARE`: `internal < 30` → the minimum (one patrol where the ceiling allows one, never more) · `< 60` 0.7 · `< 80` 0.9 · else 1.0 |

`max = clamp(floor(min(pool, ceiling) × PAY_SHARE × WILL_SHARE), min(1, ceiling), ceiling)` minus every `losses` row still within its recovery; the BAND is the ceiling's band stepped down one for `unfunded` pay or a will below 30, never below `thin` (the floor). Each gate reads its own one truth; none is a second number, and none is stored. The `thorp` row is not in the owner's list: the size ladder begins at `thorp` (`tierFacts.js`), and a thorp keeps no watch. The brief's "security band" is NOT `warSeatBooks.js`'s `securityBand` (`secure · holding · precarious`, the RULER's seat security — a registered homonym); it is `defenseLedger`'s `internal`.

### 3.2 War and belief move the count INSIDE the envelope (the owner's first word)
- **PEACE** — the town's courts at peace and nothing believed near: `fielded = ceil(max × peaceShare)`, `peaceShare = clamp(PEACE_SHARE + PEACE_POSTURE_W × (0.5 − appetite01) + PEACE_ALIGN_W × harsh01, 0.4, 1.0)` — `PEACE_SHARE` 0.6 ("less during peacetime"), the court's own risk appetite (`strategicPosture.js :: courtRiskAppetiteOf`: a wary court keeps more riders out, a bold or lax one fewer; `PEACE_POSTURE_W` 0.4) and the seat's alignment (`settlementAlignment`: a lawful-cruel seat leans to the watch, `harsh01` = lawfulness × malice; `PEACE_ALIGN_W` 0.2) — all DRAFT, UNMEASURED; where the posture layer is dark, the flat 0.6. This fork is what keeps every rider out in quiet years, and it is what §7.5's harassment prices; the stage records the first week of each unbroken run of such weeks in which the fork reads above `PEACE_SHARE` (`forkSince`, §2.7), so "held one season" is a stored fact.
- **A BELIEVED THREAT** in the territory or on a half of the round (§5.1) — `fielded` rises toward `max` by the threat's believed band (one step per band above `thin`, DRAFT).
- **AT WAR AT HOME** — the town in a live war front with its army at home, or an enemy army believed in its land: `fielded = max` for the first-response weeks (§9.1); while the town's OWN army is out, an enemy believed in the land raises `fielded` to the KEPT count (what stayed — the stamp's `riders`), never above it: the riders who marched are with the army.
- **THE ARMY ON CAMPAIGN** (the town's army deployed abroad, `deployments[id].targetId`) — MORE than in peace wherever the envelope has room, and never fewer, as the owner's first word asks ("more during wartime … if they have an army in a campaign"): the riders the court keeps are set ONCE, when the army is raised — by default `kept = max(ceil(max × CAMPAIGN_SHARE), min(max, ceil(max × peaceShare) + 1))`, `CAMPAIGN_SHARE` 0.75 (DRAFT, UNMEASURED) — so the campaign count is NEVER below the court's own peace count and is ONE MORE wherever the envelope has room (equal only where the peace count already stands at `max`) — and up to `max` where the court's chooser keeps more (`keepRiders`, §9.1(e)) (EXECUTED `cure/D-PR-r3-probe/arith.out` (1): over 60 rows the default campaign count is below the peace count in 0, equal in 15 — each with the peace count at `max` — and above in 45); the kept riders are drawn from the army's seed at that deploy (`patrolDraw`, PR3, stamped on the deployment record, §2.7), and while that army is out `fielded` never exceeds them. They ride THE ARMY'S ROAD FIRST — the halves of its path out through the town's own land, its supply road (§2.2's first priority term) — then the rest of the round in priority order; whatever halves they do not reach go UNPATROLLED, and the back roads go to the brigands (felt sentence 9). Everything not kept marched with the army (ABSORBED — written as nothing; the riders are with the army).
- **THE TOWN BESIEGED** — the riders are inside the walls: `fielded = 0` on the roads; they are part of `homeDefense` already.
- **THE DM'S ORDER** may lift `fielded` to `max` (`PATROL_ORDER` `first` on `round`) or hold it at the minimum (`avoid` on `round`) (§11.1); never beyond the envelope, and while an army is out never beyond what it left at home.

## §4 THE ONE PATROL STAGE AND THE ROUND (PR-0)

### 4.1 The stage (per tick; every preset where `patrolsEnabled` is lit) — `src/domain/patrols/patrolStage.js :: advancePatrols`
A LEAF called at the head of `calamityKernel.js :: advanceCalamity` right AFTER ST's site stage (which follows MN's menace stage; OD-MN-20, ST §6.1), before calamity's own flag gate; +3 host lines (the call and the merge of `{ worldState, newsEntries, receipts }`); zero kernel lines. Car-0 (a MEASUREMENT before any PR line): re-measure the host with MN's and ST's leaves (461 + theirs + 3 ≤ 800); MN-0's four STOP conditions inherited verbatim; ⛔ STOP at build if the host cannot carry a third leaf or if MN-0/ST-0 chose another host — then PR's stage follows theirs. Each tick, in this fixed order, every step writing only through `patrols/patrolLedger.js`:
1. **Losses that recovered** drop out of the count; losses past the horizon are dropped.
2. **The envelope and the fielded count** for every settlement (§3), codepoint order; and each town's `forkSince` (§2.7) written, kept or deleted by its peace-posture fork's run this week.
3. **Detachments advance** (§5): converging riders arrive (their leg priced by `namedPersonLegTicks`), engaged detachments resolve their hearing's verdict or default (§11.2), a hunt moves to this week's predicted place (§5.5) and, where its quarry fled by a way the town does not know whole, rolls its GLEANING (§5.5; BD's write), finished errands DISPERSE (a receipt) — a hunt also when its trail goes cold — and detachments past the rest bound send their riders `resting` (§7.3).
   - **THE RIDERS' LEG LINE FOR THE `horse` RATIO.** A detachment's leg takes WY's `horse` ratio (`ridingLegFactor(grade, riding)` = `MODE_SPEEDS.foot / MODE_SPEEDS.horse` on the `road` and `highway` GRADES, ×1 on every other grade) in its OWNER'S ONE LINE here — the pricer's owner applies the ratio; MG composes nothing and PR mints no speed — by WY's ruling ONCE (the `horse` `MODES` row, OD-MG-15, owner-signed after WY measures the hop-week distribution); until WY rules, the leg is priced unchanged.
4. **New detachments form** from believed threats (§5.2), hunts (§5.5: a pursued body believed in the land; a mission taken under a named leader), looks (§9.2), escorts (§10.4) and the DM's order — each drawing riders from the round in reverse priority (the least important stretch loses its rider first).
5. **The round, the net and the placement** — the remaining riders are placed on their halves by the round's order, the rider-time allocation and the periodic clock (§2.3: one place per rider); then, in a kingdom whose crown's own hold reads strong, THE NET (§10.3 (d)) places the crown's riders and each member's lent riders (up to its `lend(m)`, §10.3 (c)) on the halves the homes left unwatched where a need stands — after every home's own round, so a home's first call is never displaced; the stage writes the coming week's `placement` (`next` for t+1; the old `next` moved to `lived`; the coverage ring advanced) — the ONE placement every consumer of t+1 reads (PR4); and from the `lived` rows of the week just lived it writes the two facts other owners keep or read: each member's SEASON TALLY `weeksCovered` (§2.7) and, through THE BODIES' one writer `bodies/hiddenWays.js`, the `watched` mark of every stored known way whose junction half its riders stood on (BD §2.3; the mark dropped by BD's writer after a season with none).
6. **Reports** — every patrol that SAW something this week (a believed threat confirmed, a crossing witnessed, a neighbour's riders seen on the neighbour's half at a shared gate, a look's finding, a catch, a clash) emits a `patrol_report` (§9.3) — the six `finding` words, `riders_seen` (§8.3's first producer) among them.

**Clock** (requirement 9): weekly. The stage runs at `advanceCalamity`'s head (kernel l.2527) — AFTER every consumer of the week: `ageRoamingStressors` (l.510, the counter term's stamp), `advanceSettlementSupply` (l.843, banditry and smuggling), `advanceRumorLedgers` (l.2097, reports), `advanceEnvoyDiplomacyPulse` (l.2194, THE ONE PERSON STAGE's encounters), `advanceEmbattlement` (l.2391, the slow half), `advanceArmyTransit` (l.2442, the witness and the screen). So the placement it writes at tick t for t+1 is exactly the placement every consumer reads in t+1 — read-last, write-next made STRUCTURAL by the stored digest, never by re-derivation; a pin asserts that every consumer of one week names the same placement (R30). THE BODIES' stage runs AFTER this one at the same head (BD §2.7: "Bodies move AFTER the riders are placed") and reads the row whose `forTick` is ITS tick — after step 5 that row is `lived` — so a body meets the week's true watch and R30 holds for it too; PL's protection and friction rows read the SEASON TALLY the stage counts from those same `lived` rows (`bannerCoverOf`, §10.3 (f)) — PL's polity stage runs later in the same tick (`advanceTreatiesWithDisposition`, kernel l.2752, after `advanceCalamity` l.2527 — PL's measured order, `cure/D-PL-r5.md` §1 (d)), so at a season's LAST tick the tally already holds the whole season. The ordering is CONFIRMED (`cure/D-PR-r2.md` §1(f)).

### 4.2 Why weekly placement and not a stepper
A stepper would put patrols in WC's counts-mover manifest and in the frozen transit kernel's collision pass, and would make the map draw marching order the table never sees. A weekly placement is exactly the host document's own model (presence per stretch per week, by address — a phase fixed by hash), it costs one stored row per town per week, and it gives the DM the only fact that matters: which roads were watched this week. Macro over micro (the owner, 06:32).

---

## §5 REACT · GROUP · DISPERSE (the owner's first word: "react and have reasonable routes, be able to group and disperse depending on belief and effect")

### 5.1 What the town believes is out there (THE ONE THREAT READ)
`believedThreatsIn(worldState, snapshot, settlementId, tick) → [{ ref, place, band, sinceTick, source }]` — the town's belief, never the truth (K3), from the landed carriers each threat already has:
- a MENACE — MN's court picture "derived on read from the rumor records it holds (last seen where and when; the tale's might through a might↔magnitude map)" (MN §4.5); its band = the believed might;
- a DEN — a site attached to the town held by an UNTIED holder (ST §2.1: a body — a gang or a beast, THE BODIES' ref `{ kind: 'body', … }` — a menace, or a villain; never a conclave by holder kind, whose site is a church's — an `outlawed` or `curbed` flock's site is ST's SEIZURE by the court's stance, ST §6.1 step 6c, the court's own act and force, never the riders' choice) AS THE COURT BELIEVES IT — ST's one read `believedDenOf(worldState, courtId, siteId, snapshot)`, which reads the holder's own belief record and never ST's (ST: "ST's record keeps only the TRUTH of the holder"), so a den that LEFT unseen is still believed there until word comes: not under the court veil (`keptSecret`, ST §2.4 — never the map's `hidden` word), for a kept-secret den is learned only by WITNESS — a look's finding (§9.2 (b)) or the warden's ways (§5.5) — and makes no `den_unanswered`;
- a BODY — THE BODIES' gang, conclave or beast the town has heard of: LISTED by BD's `believedBodies(worldState, observerId)` → `{ bodyId, bodyKind, lastWhereId, lastWhat, lastTick }` and READ, each, through the town's ONE belief record of it, `believedBodyOf(worldState, townId, bodyId)` → `{ known, mightBand?, baseId?, lastWhereId?, lastWhat?, lastTick?, leaderRef?, source }` (BD §2.4: from the town's rumor records carrying the body's id and the sightings registered as rows of the same source — a site's sighting, a patrol's report, an INFORMER's word where the commons fear the body, a caught operative's confession; truth in the omniscient arm) — its band the believed `mightBand`, its last place and tick the record's; a THREAT the riders ride against only where the court's stance toward it is `hunted` (a gang) or `outlawed` (a conclave), or it is a beast (§5.5; BD §7.1) — a body the court lets be is no threat to ride at; WC-12's located brigand body IS BD's gang record (BD's route to WC);
- a HOSTILE ARMY near — an army the town has witnessed in its land or on a stretch of its round (PS's crossing rows with `witnessed`; the war's own front reads);
- a CROSSING UNIT — a PS crossing in the town's land in state `trespassing` or `overstayed`;
- a REPORT — any `patrol_report` or rumor telling the town holds placing such a thing on its land, true or FALSE (IN's plant road — the `plant_taken` / `lure_sprung` kinds IN registers (IN-2), a lie that "needs a liar" with a traceable source (PT §11); a provocateur's staged report rides the same road (CH)).
Under the omniscient arm (no rumor road) the read returns the truth inside the town's territory (every belief read does); false reports exist only where the rumor road runs (infoMode `full`) — the TIER line says so.

### 5.2 Converge and group
For each believed threat, in descending believed band (codepoint tie-break): if one patrol's band ≥ the threat's believed band, ONE rider leaves the round (`converging`, then `engaged`); else the town forms a DETACHMENT of the fewest riders whose summed latent strength reaches the threat's band (each band's latent points read from MN's ONE table `MIGHT_LATENT` — `thin` 13 · `modest` 34 · `capable` 51 · `strong` 69 · `formidable` 89, each band's midpoint on the 0..100 latent scale (MN §5.2; THE BODIES' `bodyStrength` reads the same) — summed and re-banded on `STRENGTH_BANDS`' floors), drawing from the round in reverse priority, and never more than `fielded − 1` (one rider always stays on the round's first half) — EXCEPT a ONE-WEEK act (a look; a response to a threat within one hop of the seat), which may take the town's last rider at the round's cost, so a hamlet's one rider can look and a village's two can ride together against what is at its door. If the whole fielded count cannot reach the threat's band, the riders DO NOT GROUP TO FIGHT: one rider watches and reports, and the town's believed threat stands for its court's own deciders (the chooser, relief, the menace's response — §6's consumers read it). Arrival takes `namedPersonLegTicks({ nominalWeeks: hopWeeks(digest, seat, place, season) })` weeks, `UNPRICED_LEG_WEEKS` per graph hop aspatially.

### 5.3 What a detachment does when it arrives (one contest each, never PR's own law)
| The threat | The act | The contest (its owner) |
|---|---|---|
| a den in a site | `clear` | ST's `site_cleared` (CLIMAX; "the force's strength (its `offensive`, or the patrol's band) against the holder's might on `SIEGE_VERDICT_BANDS`, one keyed draw") — ST §6.1 step 6 already names "a grouped patrol" as a clearer |
| a menace | report and withdraw, unless the detachment's band ≥ the menace's might — then `clear` | MN's `menace_met` (CLIMAX; options `engage · withdraw`, then `menace_beaten · army_beaten · indecisive`) with the detachment as the force — a PRODUCER ROW in MN's commit (MN §7's trigger list gains "a patrol detachment's arrival"); MN's `army_beaten` reads "an army or a detachment" — one vocabulary, MN's |
| a BODY its court pursues — a `hunted` gang, an `outlawed` conclave, a beast (THE BODIES; WC-12's located brigands are BD's gangs) — on the road, a trail the town knows, or in the country | `pursue` (THE HUNT, §5.5) | `patrol_engages` (§11.2) — PR's band contest on `SIEGE_VERDICT_BANDS` with BD's `bodyStrength` as the foe's force, one keyed draw, PR's one vocabulary (BD §9.3: "PR's `patrol_engages` — `driven_off · screened · withdrew · cut_down`, PR's band contest, PR's card") — never WC-12's siege words (`suppression · worn_down · absorbed · buy_off`, WC-12's `EMBATTLED_RESOLUTIONS`, `cure/L-BD.md` §1(a)), which close a brigand SIEGE, never a road fight |
| an enemy army marching on the town, or crossing its land toward another | `screen` | `patrol_engages` (§9.1) — PR hosts the screen's card in either case; PS keeps the home-defence battle |
| a unit crossing the town's land (PS) | witness and escort only (PS's ladder answers it) | PS's cards; a `barred` answer's contest is PS's (§10.4) |
| nothing (the threat was never there) | look, find nothing, disperse | none — a `patrol_report` "found nothing" (§9.3) |

### 5.4 Disperse
A detachment DISPERSES — its riders return to `on_round` the next week, a `patrol_dispersed` receipt with `causedBy` the finishing verdict or report — when its contest resolves, when the threat is no longer believed (the town's belief has moved: a later report, the rumor's TTL, the threat gone — a hunt's COLD TRAIL is one, §5.5), when the DM's order ends it, or at the rest bound (§7.3).

### 5.5 THE HUNT — riders after a body (the owner's ~19:0x word: "patrols when not in routine patrolling are trying to predict and catch up to gangs, beasts, and hostile conclaves"; THE BODIES §9.3, which cites this section; L-BD items 20 · 39 · 40 · 41)
- **WHAT IS HUNTED.** A town's riders pursue a body ONLY where the town's court stance toward it (BD's `courtStanceOf`, BD §7.1) is `hunted` (a gang) or `outlawed` (a conclave), or it is a beast (every court hunts beasts, BD10). A `permitted` or `endowed` conclave and a `tolerated`, `employed` or `harboured` gang are NEVER pursued (the owner: a good conclave in a good town is "not … pursued by patrols"); a `curbed` conclave is WATCHED, never hunted — met and noticed, it is reported (§8.1), not engaged. The stance is the court's, decided in its own hearing (BD's `body_stance`); the riders never choose it. A GANG's default is `hunted` — `tolerated` only where the town's effective tolerance reads ≥ 0.3 or its underworld rules, a patron-less town taking its law from its government (BD §7.1: "OTHERWISE → `hunted` (the owner, ~18:3x: "These are public enemies to most")") — so the riders pursue most gangs, and a deity-free world's towns are no safe road.
- **THE PREDICTION, ON BELIEF.** A pursued body the town believes in or beside its land (§5.1) is ridden for at its PREDICTED place, `predictedPlaceOf(worldState, snapshot, settlementId, bodyId, tick)` (§2.6), from three things the town holds and nothing it does not: (i) its newest sighting — where and what, from its ONE belief record of the body (`believedBodyOf`'s `lastWhereId`, `lastWhat`, `lastTick`; an INFORMER's word where the town's commons fear the body is one of its rows, BD §7.6: "the court's belief sharpens and the hunt's prediction improves (PR's `predictedPlaceOf` reads the sighting)"); (ii) the ways it KNOWS (`knownTrailsOf`, THE BODIES' one union of the learned set and the office's ways) — an unknown way is never predicted; (iii) the body's HABITS, BD's closed `BODY_HABITS` read (its leaf `bodies/bodyHabits.js`, BD-5; BD §9.3: "a gang returns to its den, a pack follows the season, a flock goes where the faith is fractured") — THIS body's own habits where HB carries the body grain (L-BD 39 (c): "the patrol's PREDICTION … reads THIS body's habits (not the kind's)"), else its kind's. Never the body's true position (K3); `null` (nothing to go on) ⇒ no hunt, and the town waits for word.
- **THE PURSUIT.** A detachment with aim `pursue` and `pursuedRef: { kind: 'body', id }` (§2.5) forms by §5.2's rule — one rider whose band reaches the body's believed might, else the fewest riders whose summed latent points reach it, drawn from the round in reverse priority — and each week rides to the predicted place on the light-unit physics (`namedPersonLegTicks`; one place a week, PR1). The CATCH is the ONE encounter law on TRUTH (§8.1's body arm): MET only if the body is really there and the riders stand on that road half, that known trail's junction, that site or that country; NOTICED by the law's draw against the body's cover. Met and noticed ⇒ `patrol_engages` (§11.2), the house's band contest with BD's `bodyStrength`. A catch on a way TEACHES the town the way (`by_catch`, BD §2.3's writer).
- **THE WAYS, FOR RIDERS — one seam, one price.** A detachment rides a hidden way as THE HOLDER'S OWN UNIT, admitted by the HOLDER'S KNOWLEDGE READ — never by a traveller token: master's franchise "fails CLOSED" (`routeNetworkConsumers.js :: mayUseHiddenPaths`: "a lane that forgets to declare itself gets the army's answer") and PR never passes it a kind for a way; PR's mover admits a way-mode hop (`routeEdgeId(a, b, 'way')`) on its own leaf by `knownTrailsOf(worldState, snapshot, townId, tick).get(trailKey)` (the owner's 18:33 word — "a warden … has access to hidden routes network as part of their job" — and 22:19 — "knowledge of a hidden path can be gleaned from a regular body if in pursuit of an enemy"), ONLY for a `pursue` detachment within the way's capacity (`modest`, BD §5.2: "a body of thirty, never a column") on a way its town KNOWS — the office's ways whole, a learned way as far as its `upTo` — every other patrol move admitted to no way (the round, a screen, an escort, a look and every larger detachment take the road: "armies may not" stands for them); a way ridden is priced at the landed `gradeLegMultiplier('hidden')` 2 (= `HIDDEN_PATH_SLOWDOWN` 2, "PINNED" equal) on the one leg physics, never a factor of PR's; a patrol NEVER CROSSES a border by a way (PS §5.5: "a patrol never holds a transit clock, and it never takes a hidden way" — its crossing beyond its land is the stretch's, through PS's reads); the way's rung is the route ledger's and PR's riding adds no use of its own (THE BODIES' use counter is the bodies') — PLAUSIBLE until PR-2's car-0 measures that PR's leg leaf can carry the hidden kind (its legs are `namedPersonLegTicks` over `hopWeeks`, H3's `planWanderLeg` the landed precedent with "`gradeMultiplier: hop.hidden ? NPC_CONSEQUENCES_TUNING.HIDDEN_PATH_SLOWDOWN : 1,`").
- **GLEANING IN PURSUIT (L-BD 11e; BD §2.3: "THE PATROLS' gleaning roll, PR's text, BD's write").** A `pursue` detachment whose quarry FLED BY A WAY its town does not know whole — the body truly left the place the riders reach by a way (truth: the sign is on the ground, as the encounter is) — rolls ONCE per pursuit week on the stream `bodies:glean:<unitKey>:<trailKey>:<tick>` (BD's stream name; `unitKey = 'patrol:' + detachmentId`, PS §2.1's key) to learn the way NODE BY NODE: `p = clamp(GLEAN_BASE × sign × ground × season × craft × home × length × told × habit, 0, GLEAN_CAP)` — SIGN, the quarry's might band and HASTE (a fleeing body leaves more); GROUND, the way's cover (`cover01`: forest and wetland hide, mountain passes show); SEASON (snow shows tracks, high summer hides them — the one seasonal read); CRAFT, a leader of THE WARDEN ARCHETYPE reads sign a levy cannot (the office's person, below); HOME, the riders on their own country (their own `lived` placement; a kingdom's riders on a member's land count as home only under a strong hold, §10.3 (d)); LENGTH, the weeks the pursuit has run; TOLD, a captive's or an informer's word (BD §7.6); HABIT, the riders' own lessons on HB's curve where it lights — every factor DRAFT, UNMEASURED (`GLEAN_BASE` 0.3, `GLEAN_CAP` 0.8); a success writes `by_pursuit` through BD's one writer `bodies/hiddenWays.js` with the PREFIX reached (`upTo`, one node further a success — "followed as far as the ford"), the knowledge then fading on HB's curve as BD §2.3 writes it; the receipt names what was learned and why ("Kell's riders learned the drover's way as far as the ford: snow on the ground, a gang in haste"). A town that BOUGHT the way from a broker first has nothing to glean (`by_broker`, L-BD 11g) (the base and the cap are a refinement, vetoable).
- **THE COLD TRAIL.** When the town's newest sighting is older than `COLD_TRAIL_WEEKS` (DRAFT 3, UNMEASURED) — `tick − lastTick`, read from `believedBodyOf`, so nothing is stored for the dwell — the trail is COLD: the detachment DISPERSES (§5.4) and the round resumes. A sighting by a shadowing rider, a report, a raid heard of — any fresh word — keeps it warm.
- **THE UNRIDDEN ROUND (the first counterforce).** The hunters are off the round: the halves they walked fall to `never` while they ride (§4.1 step 4 takes them from the bottom of the round), and a body that plans on `believedWatch` (§8.3) and has heard the riders went hunting slips through the gap — the gang's chance, by belief only (BD12).
- **SHADOW AND GROUP (the second).** A rider OUTMATCHED by the body's believed might never engages: it SHADOWS — follows the prediction and reports (`patrol_report` `threat_seen`, §9.3), keeping the trail warm — while the town groups (§5.2) or asks an ally (`patrol_joins`, §10.1 (d)); under a strong banner a fellow member's riders may ride to it by right, still that member's decision on a `patrol_joins` card (the banner spares the crossing, never the choice — §10.3 (d)).
- **OVER-PURSUIT (the third).** A hunt is bounded by the rest bound (§7.3) and the cold trail; beyond the town's border it is PS's ladder (an ally welcomes hot pursuit as urgency, §10.1 (c)) or the banner's right (§10.3 (d)); a hunt that rides into a body stronger than it believed is `cut_down` (§7.2) — the price of a wrong belief.
- **THE HUNT LED (L-BD 40; the owner's word 12).** When a mission to hunt a NAMED gang or flock, or a beast pack (BD §9.11: "a pack's threat read by its BELIEVED HARM in the court's land, not by fame"), is TAKEN — its card is THE BODIES' `hunt_taken` (BD §9.11: "TAKING THE MISSION is BD's card `hunt_taken` … never PR's"; STANDING until its one CLIMAX week, the mission's close, and closed early by the DM's act; lit with `bodyEffectsEnabled`) — PR's detachment is its unit (the quarry rule above binds a mission too — a `curbed` conclave is never hunted, BD §7.1; BD §9.11 reads L-BD 40's "`suppressed`/`outlawed`" as `outlawed` alone, PR's route applied there): sized to the body's believed band PLUS ONE ("appropriate to scale"; DRAFT, UNMEASURED), never more than `fielded − 1` (one rider stays on the round's first half), and short of it the hunt shadows and asks (above); its `leaderRef` names the leader the card names — by BD §9.11's order: (1) the town's PUBLIC-SECURITY office — a person of THE WARDEN ARCHETYPE holding it, else the sword-holder by L-BD 23's one read (BD's `SWORD_BY_GOVERNMENT` names the faction, the office the person), else the watch's senior seat — (2) a court's retained paragon (CH), (3) a free paragon answering a bounty (CH; a CORPS answering a bounty hunts with its OWN unit, never PR's riders). PR NEVER MOVES A PERSON'S RECORD (CH's law, "two movers never share a record"): a PARAGON leader moves by its OWN mover — CH's purpose `hunt_body` with the body as its target (CH §4.2: "`hunt_body` (not `hunt`, landed five quoted-literal files and THE BODIES' court↔beast stance)"), reading the ONE prediction `predictedPlaceOf` on the one physics, so leader and riders stand together — and its lean on the riders (`paragonLean(…, 'wildland', 'patrol_rider_quality')`, §10.5) applies only in a week it stands with them; an office-holder who is not a paragon is a REFERENCE on the row — its name on the card, its recorded axes on the hunt's two forks (a cautious leader shadows unless a band ahead and keeps a trail a week longer; a reckless one engages at an equal band and rides past the rest bound — over-pursuit as its price; DRAFT ±1, UNMEASURED; the person-habit curve where HB carries the person grain, L-BD 39) — and its residency never moves (macro over micro). THE LEADER NEVER DIES BY THE ENGINE (no named fate): a `cut_down` verdict writes the riders' `losses` row and leaves the leader untouched; the mission's outcome words are its card's (L-BD 40: the body's and the unit's, never the person's), and a paragon's failure is CH's `paragon_disgraced` hearing. ONE HUNT, ONE DEED ("the hearing's OWNER mints: a LED hunt's success is ONE deed minted by the hunt's own door — CH's `hunt_won` at the mission's close, doer the LEADER (`ledBy`), target the body; PR's `patrol_clash` mints NO deed for a led hunt (its row is the clash's record)"): the riders' clashes inside a led hunt are `patrol_engages` verdicts and nothing more; the mission's close mints `hunt_won` (CH's door) or, a hunt that fails as `escaped`, the body's own deed (RN registers it) — PR mints neither. A hunt's coin is never the treasury's `deployments` upkeep: a detachment is no deployment record (`treasury.js :: computeUpkeep` charges `{ ledger, record }` over "`UPKEEP_LEDGERS = Object.freeze(['deployments'])`" only, "the `deployments` one-army ledger"), so its cost is the round it leaves unridden and the riders' own pay (§3.1's PAY gate; the W-COIN `patrols` upkeep row when it lights, §7.1) — a menace-scale COLUMN is a deployment and pays as one (BD §9.11's general).
- **THE WARDEN KNOWS THE WAYS (L-BD 41; the owner's word 13).** Where the town's public-security office is HELD by a person of THE WARDEN ARCHETYPE — for a paragon, the one CH returns as the office's holder, `securityOfficeOf(worldState, courtId)` (CH P1 `heldOffice`, the DM's appointment), then CH's one predicate `isWardenParagon` on it (CH §2.2: "`domain === 'wildland'` on the champion side of its LIVING polarity"), for an office-holder who is no paragon its landed roster role `warden` (`generators/factionRoles.js`: "`warden: 'watch'`"), never the display noun — the town's KNOWN ways include every hidden way of the office's JURISDICTION — the home's land, and for a crown's office the kingdom's by the crown's reach (§10.3 (c)) — the OFFICE's knowledge, DERIVED while the office is so held and never stored (a new warden knows on taking the post; a noble or a militia master without the trade knows only what the town has LEARNED, BD §2.3's stored half), and NEVER a warden merely RETAINED by the court who does not hold the office ("the office knowledge belongs to the person HOLDING the public-security office with the warden archetype (L-BD 41), never to any retained warden"); the union is ONE read, BD's `knownTrailsOf`. On PR's side: (a) a WARDEN-LED hunt may ride the ways — under the riders' franchise above (the office's ways whole, at the landed hidden price), the detachment no larger than the way's capacity (`modest`, BD §5.2: "a body of thirty, never a column"), else it keeps to the road and the junctions; (b) the town's riders cover the junctions of every way the office knows (§2.2: the office's ways' junction halves FIRST), and a body that has heard of the warden believes those ways watched (`believedWatch` on a trail key, §8.3) — deterrence by belief: "Kell's warden knows the drover's way; the hill gang has moved its den across the border."
- **CLOCK · TIER · COUNTERFORCE · FELT.** Weekly (the stage, §4.1); `bodiesEnabled` for the quarry, `bodyEffectsEnabled` for a LED hunt (BD's `hunt_taken` and its detachment), `hiddenWaysEnabled` for the ways (dark: every body is on the road, and a hunt rides roads and countries only), `routeLifecycleEnabled` only for a way's RUNG to move (dark: the candidates alone at the hidden price, BD §5.2's degraded arm); belief presets carry the prediction on heard sightings, the omniscient arm on the truth in the town's land (no false trail exists there); the counterforces above; felt — "Kell's riders caught the Tolle gang on the drovers' path; the watch knows the way now" (surface), "Kell's riders lost the Tolle gang in the forest and came home" (surface), and on scrutiny the prediction's three reasons.

---

## §6 WHAT IT CHANGES — bounded, one truth each (items 5, 16, 17; PR5, PR6)

Every effect below is ONE lean at ONE named seam, behind `patrolEffectsEnabled` (§12); every factor DRAFT, UNMEASURED, FP-46 roster; absent the flag every host is byte-identical.

### 6.1 THE SLOW HALF — the presence subtraction (item 17(a))
**Seam:** MN §3.3's ONE composition sentence, called inside `embattlement.js :: advanceEmbattlement` before the step and living in its NEUTRAL home `spatial/presenceComposer.js :: presenceTerms` (MN §3.3: "whose home is neutral, `src/domain/spatial/presenceComposer.js :: presenceTerms`"); PR's subtraction is ONE row of that module's closed registration list (the σ slot), never a line of `embattlement.js` — THE SHARED PRESENCE COMPOSER, an OWNED unit, MN's (MN §3.3: "the composer is an OWNED unit — one manifest row, its owner volume THIS one … cut with whichever of C14 · C15 · C16 · C10 is cut first, never re-implemented by a caller — and it RESOLVES THE PHYSICS, so it reads TRUTH"): whichever of C14 · C15 · C16 · C10 is cut first carries MN's unit and its manifest row, and every other REGISTERS its feeder rows; it lights when ANY registered feeder's own flag is lit, each feeder gated by its own flag, and `menacesEnabled` gates only MN's feeder rows — so PR's subtraction needs no menace, and if C16 is cut first MN's composer unit is cut WITH PR-1 (MN's text and manifest row, never a PR re-implementation; zero kernel lines — the host receives `worldState`): `presenceTerms` — per town the MAX over the feeders, capped once at `PRESENCE_TERM_CAP`, then the ONE suppressor slot (ST's sites and PR's riders, one shared cap) on the SUPPRESSIBLE part only, never on the walls term. PR does not restate that sentence; it supplies one term to the suppressor slot. **PR's term:** `patrolSubtraction(T) = PATROL_SUB_MAX × coverageOf(T)`, `PATROL_SUB_MAX` 0.15, where `coverageOf` is the mean of the town's four-week coverage ring (§2.6 — the SLOW signal, never this week's presence alone; when the levy marches, the subtraction fades over four weeks). What is suppressible is MN's sentence (MN §3.3): `roadCountry` ONLY — never the walls term and never an enemy column (PS's feeder, widened to the destination's own land) — so PR's riders lower a raided country's brigand-like danger and never an armed host's (MN: "an armed host is answered by the siege verdict and relief, or by the war's own contest and the screen"). A menace or an army at the walls is never suppressed; a RAIDING menace may be taken below the latch by sites and riders together — "the counterforce WORKING" (MN's M50). It lowers only what feeders ADDED, so a calm town's roads gain nothing (the host document's "peacetime roads carry no danger to relieve"). Against the latch the riders work by its own hysteresis (`embattlement.js :: stepEmbattlement`: "`} else if (rawT < T.EXIT_THRESHOLD && (now - priorSince) >= T.MIN_DWELL_TICKS) {`", `EXIT_THRESHOLD` 0.3, `MIN_DWELL_TICKS` 6): a CALM watched country may never ENTER the latch, an embattled one LEAVES only when its suppressed presence stays below 0.30 for the dwell — never while a latch-sized menace raids (its raid part less the shared cap still reads 0.35, MN M53: the level eases toward it and the town stays embattled) and never while an army is at the walls or in the land (never suppressed). **Every danger consumer follows by construction** (§15.1): route choice (`scoreRoute`/`chooseRoute`), realized banditry (`routeDangerLevel`), the roads gauntlet's T3 and the sea's S3 (both read `embattlementLevel`), migration road deaths, charters. **Tier:** the canon marker only (`embattlementActive`); aspatial worlds have no danger arm.

### 6.2 THE COUNTER TERM — the raider row and the menace's wear (the fold's ruling, kept)
`patrolCounterTerm(T) = clamp(K_COUNTER × (coverageOf(T) − referenceCoverage(tier(T))), −0.10, +0.10)`, `K_COUNTER` 0.25 — the DEVIATION of the town's actual watch from the REFERENCE watch of its tier (the coverage a `funded` town of that tier with `internal` 60 fields at peace). **ONE entry, one value, reaching two readers:** the term enters ST's ONE additive post-blend slot after `counterforceAssessment` for `monster_raider_pressure` (ST §3.3's raider counterforce term; sites and patrols SUM inside it and take ONE clamp, ST's ±0.15) — through ST's leaf `siteTermAt`, so `stressorDynamics.js` (759) takes no PR line — and per stressor it is the FOOTPRINT MEAN of the per-town terms over `stressor.affectedSettlementIds`, the landed blend's own averaging (`counterforceAssessment`: "`const rawScore = clamp01(scoreSum / entries.length);`"), inside that one clamp. A raiding MENACE's wear takes the term only THROUGH THE STAMP (`stressors.js :: ageRoamingStressors` stamps "`counterforce: { score: … }`", the score MN §5.5's wear read takes — MN's slot (2) "FILLED THROUGH THE STAMP, never added here"); PR adds no row to `menaceCounterTerms`, so the riders are never counted twice in a menace's wear; the one fallback is MN's — should ST-1's car-0 STOP fire, MN reads `siteTermAt`'s raider row directly instead (MN §5.5). THE CORRIDOR: the same leaf's second row, for `criminal_corridor`, is `K_CORRIDOR × coverageOf(T)` (DRAFT 0.10, UNMEASURED) — RAW coverage, not a deviation, because the corridor's landed counterforce has NO watch term to deviate from (`STRESSOR_COUNTERFORCES.criminal_corridor`: `criminal_opportunity` (invert) 0.4 · `legitimacy` 0.3 · `labor_capacity` 0.3); PR-1's car-0 measures it. So the riders WEAR a raiding menace and shorten ordinary raider and smuggling stressors where they ride more than a town of that size usually does, and LENGTHEN them where the watch has gone to the war, gone unpaid or been cut down ("With the watch gone to the war, the hill road has gone to the brigands" — and where a held keep stands on that road, "… the hill road beyond the keep's reach …"; the keep's own rows stay). The landed counterforce already reads `institution defense` 0.3 and the generated `defense_readiness` 0.5, which ARE the standing watch; adding raw coverage would count the same riders twice. The deviation adds only what the patrols DO beyond the institutions' standing presence; PR-5 measures its correlation with the landed terms (§14 PR-5) and, if ρ > 0.7 on the soak corpus, the reference narrows to the war-and-belief deviation alone.

### 6.3 THE FAST HALF — presence this week lowers the incident roll (item 17(b))
**Seam:** `embattlement.js :: banditryLoss({ channelStrength, dangerLevel, rng, fireLevel? })` — ONE optional input: the FIRE TEST reads `fireLevel` when present (`draw >= fireLevel × BANDITRY_CHANCE`); the LOSS SIZE keeps reading `dangerLevel` (the unwatched mean — "`const frac = (T.BANDITRY_MIN_LOSS + draw2 * (…)) * danger;`"), so the fast half makes an attack rarer, never smaller; absent ⇒ byte-identical (+2 lines; host 172). **The per-hop watched level** (a leaf, `patrols/patrolEffects.js :: watchedFireLevel(route, worldState, tick)`): `fireLevel = clamp01( Σ_{i≥1} embattlementLevel(path[i]) × m_i / hops )` — hop `i` is `path[i−1] → path[i]` (the landed `scoreRoute` sum, danger per traversed node), with `m_i = 1 − WATCH_CUT × watchOn(road{path[i−1], path[i]}, tick) × (1 − slowShare(path[i]))` on a LAND hop and `m_i = 1` on a SEA hop (no naval damper — `navalLayer.js`: "no naval-patrol damper") — a per-hop multiplier on that hop's danger BEFORE the route's mean, never a route-wide MAX, so one rider on one road cuts only its own hop's share of a ten-hop route; `WATCH_CUT` 0.5 (DRAFT, UNMEASURED). **Callers** pass `fireLevel` beside `dangerLevel = routeDangerLevel(route)`: `commodityFlow.js` (+1; 474) and `supplyShipments.js` (+1; 210). **THE JOINT COMPOSITION** (item 17's "never doubled" — one composition per consumer):

| Consumer | The slow half (presence, §6.1) | The fast half (this week) | The joint bound |
|---|---|---|---|
| goods banditry (`banditryLoss`) | lowers the node level ⇒ attacks RARER and SMALLER (the size is `frac × danger`) | lowers the FIRE level on the watched hop only ⇒ RARER, never smaller | the fast cut on a hop is scaled by `1 − slowShare(node)` — `slowShare` = the share of that node's suppressible presence this week's patrol subtraction removed (the composer's own two numbers; a proxy for the ramped level, UNMEASURED) — so where the riders' slow half already holds a node their fast half adds proportionally less; THE JOINT FLOOR is an assertion PR-5 measures on the soak corpus: on a watched embattled hop the expected loss stays ≥ `JOINT_FLOOR` (DRAFT 0.25, UNMEASURED) × the same hop's expected loss with no riders; a breach lowers `WATCH_CUT`, never adds a rule |
| named travellers on roads missions (T3, S3) | through `embattlementLevel`, by construction | none — ruled (§17): the slow half alone now; the fast half is a roads leaf-first car on C7 | the slow half alone |
| a held fort's road effects (ST §3.3) | the keep's OWN suppression (0.20) STANDS while it is held and funded, lit or dark — its garrison is the site's own complement, never the town's riders (OD-PR-41) — and the riders' subtraction is a DISTINCT feeder of the SAME capped suppressor: `σ = min(0.25, σ_site + σ_patrol)` (MN §3.3) | the riders' own; the keep has no fast half | no fourth term and no double count (different men): the two suppressions share the ONE 0.25 cap, the keep's raider counterforce (+0.15) and the riders' deviation share ST's ONE clamp (±0.15), and the keep's three gate rows stand (PR mints none, PR5) |

 **Never a write** to the embattlement level (PR6). **Named travellers** (envoys, captives, liaisons on roads missions): their T3 / S3 rolls live in the frozen `roadsKernel.js` (838, baselined) — ruled (§17): the slow half alone; the fast half a C7 slot. **Timing a departure** (item 17's merchant): a merchant reads `believedWatch` (§8.3) — the believed presence, never the placement — so a court that HAS riders' reports can time a caravan by them; the open road's chooser (EC-18) and WY's caravans consume the one read in their own commits.

### 6.4 THE CATCH OF GOODS — smuggling (item 16)
**Seam:** `smuggle.js :: smuggleSuccessChance({ network, corruption, goodsResistance, boldness, watch01? })` — ONE optional input at the route's WORST gate, inside `raw` BEFORE the clamp: `chance = clamp(raw − WATCH_W × watch01 × BRIBE_KEEP[pay], 0, SUCCESS_MAX) × attemptFactor(boldness)` (the landed "`return round4(clamp(raw, 0, T.SUCCESS_MAX) * attemptFactor(boldness));`"), `WATCH_W` 0.25 — so the fall is `WATCH_W × watch01 × BRIBE_KEEP × attemptFactor` where the clamp does not bind, and nothing at the floor; `BRIBE_KEEP`: `unfunded` 0.4 · `meagre` 0.7 · `funded` 0.95 · `lavish` 1.0 — an unpaid watch takes the bribe (the wages band, the owner's sixth word's evasion; the corruption web's idiom as a lean) (+2 lines; host 119). **Caller:** `commodityFlow.js`'s M7 block passes `watch01 = watchOf({ road: road{prev(worst), worst.id}, holder: worst.id }, tick, worst.id)` — the WORST GATE TOWN's OWN riders on its half of the road into it (+1; the half-keyed read), never a power-blind MAX: the gate keeps the gate (J385), so neither the destination's own riders nor an ally's raise a hostile gate's catch. A caught shipment goes through the LANDED pipeline — `smugglePipeline`: "(detected ∧ hostile) SEIZURE → (elif contraband) CONFISCATION" with `seizureTake`'s conscience gate — never a second seizure law; the stance that makes goods contraband is EC-6's (`open · taxed · restricted · closed`, the crown's embargo one input — PL), the toll the gate's (J385: the settlement whose land a path crosses keeps the gate "with its tolls, interception and contraband"). A SMUGGLER as a PERSON (a villain's runner, CH's network) is a named traveller: §8's encounter, CH's ban/gate words on a catch, and a deed through RN's door when the catch is made (`patrol_catch` source row, §9.3). A patrol away (looking, converging) catches nothing (its stretch reads 0). A smuggler's RUN ON A HIDDEN WAY (L-BD 11b; BD §5.2: the landed `smuggler` franchise "is caught only on a KNOWN and WATCHED way (PR's trail arm)") skips the gate — its toll and its stance — and is watched ONLY by the trail arm: `watch01 = trailWatchOf(worldState, snapshot, holder, trailKey, tick)` of each holder in whose land one of the way's junctions lies (§2.6), `BRIBE_KEEP` the same; the run's path choice is EC's gauntlet's (BD's route to EC), the watch PR's (PLAUSIBLE until EC's commit names the way-run's seam) — the RUN is landed (`routeNetworkConsumers.js :: HIDDEN_PATH_KINDS` admits the smuggler; EC summons it), the WATCH at EC's gauntlet is recorded by ONE fold §3.1 EC INBOX row, DARK until EC names it. **THE UNPAID-WAGES SLOT (item 16) is also a GANG's mark:** THE BODIES' gang choice `bribe` (BD §4.2: "a watch whose wages go unpaid (THE PATROLS' item 16 slot: PR's riders unpaid …)") targets this slot — the town's riders' PAY band below `funded` (`unfunded` or `meagre`: `BRIBE_KEEP` 0.4 · 0.7, read from ST's `settlementFundingBand`, §3.1) — and its effect is BD's alone: the court's stance one step toward `tolerated` at its next `body_stance` fork and the gang's standing there (BD §4.2, §7.3) — NEVER a second cut on PR's notice, whose stance factor already reads the court's stance (§8.1). The brief's "the roads gauntlet's INTERCEPTION odds … the landed contraband outcome" is measured otherwise: the gauntlet rolls hazards for MISSIONS only; goods are intercepted in `smuggle.js` at the worst gate, which is where the riders' lean lands.

### 6.5 THE WITNESS (PR10) — `witnessedCrossing`
`witnessedCrossing(worldState, holderId, gateKey, unit, tick) = holderEyesOf(worldState, snapshot, { road: gateKey, holder: holderId }, tick) > 0 (the holder's EYES on its own half of the gate's road this week — its own riders, or a net rider standing there by right, PR10 — the placement; the half-keyed read) ∨ the crossing's place is the holder's seat (`settlement:<B>`: a landing or a circle arrival) ∨ sizeBand(unit) ∈ {pressing, decisive} ∨ siteWitnesses(worldState, stretch) ∋ holderId (a held watchtower, ST17)` for a road or seat place; for a TRAIL place `trail:<a|b>` it is `trailWatchOf(worldState, snapshot, holderId, trailKey, tick) > 0` ALONE (the holder KNOWS the way — `knownTrailsOf` — and its eyes stand on one of the way's junction halves this week). With `patrolsEnabled` dark it answers `true` (PS8's conservative arm). Witnessing is TRUTH (a structural fact of whether the holder saw it); what the holder believes about the unit's purpose stays PS3's claim-and-belief law. A watched stretch defeats the quiet crossing; a grouped detachment on a stretch notices what a lone rider might miss only through §8.1's notice odds (for persons), never for an army (an army is seen when it is met).

### 6.6 THE DRAW (PR3) — `patrolDraw`
**Seam: the opener, BEFORE its target loop.** In `warDeployment.js :: evaluateWarLayer` (685 eff) the court's capacity is read once — "`const fromCap = capacityFor(fromId);`" — and then judged TWICE before any seed: the coalition gate ("`classifyFeasibility({ attackerCurrent: fromCap.offensive, …`") and, inside the solo target loop, the `CONQUEST_MARGIN` prefilter ("`fromStrength * casusMult * mediation <= strengthFor(targetId) + CONQUEST_MARGIN`") and the hard gate ("`attackerCurrent: fromCap.offensive`"); only then is the record seeded ("`seedDeploymentState({ targetId: chosenTarget, cap: fromCap, …`"). So the draw is taken RIGHT AFTER `fromCap` — ONE host line, `const drawn = patrolDrawnCap(worldState, snapshot, fromId, fromCap)` (+1; the riders kept depend on the envelope and the court's posture, never on the target) — and the four reads take the drawn army in place, with no further lines: both `classifyFeasibility` calls read `drawn.cap.offensive`, the prefilter reads `fromStrength × drawn.drawShare01` (`fromStrength` is `buildStrengthLookup`'s `settlementStrength` index, a different scale, so the draw enters it as the same SHARE; PLAUSIBLE that the index scales with `offensive`, PR-5 measures), and the seed reads `cap: drawn.cap`. Inside the leaf: `points = Math.min(patrolDrawOf(worldState, snapshot, fromId, kept), fromCap.offensive)` — the strength ACTUALLY drawn; `drawn.cap = points > 0 ? { ...fromCap, offensive: fromCap.offensive − points } : fromCap` (the same object when nothing is drawn); `drawShare01 = drawn.cap === fromCap ? 1 : (fromCap.offensive > 0 ? drawn.cap.offensive / fromCap.offensive : 0)` — 1 whenever nothing was drawn, the 0/0 guard reading 0 (unreachable once `points > 0` implies a positive offensive), a draw that takes the whole army reading 0 (the share is taken over the points ACTUALLY drawn, so a court with no offensive to draw from reads 1 and keeps the landed prefilter: EXECUTED `cure/D-PR-r4-probe/drawshare.out` — the hard gate admits a zero-offensive attacker against a collapsing defender ("attacker 0 · defender 40 · legitimacy 10 → require_betrayal · permits siege true", `feasibilityGate.js :: classifyFeasibility`'s collapse override precedes every floor), so a literal 0 on an undrawn zero army would skip a target the dark arm keeps although no rider was drawn; the 0 holds for every draw that takes the whole army ("offensive 5 · draw 12 → … drawShare01 0"), and the stamp's `points` never exceed the strength drawn — vetoable: the alternative not taken is the literal 0/0 → 0 with `points` uncapped). The seeded record carries the conditional `patrolDraw: { riders, points }` stamp by ONE line beside the seed, written WHENEVER RIDERS ARE KEPT — `points` 0 where there was nothing to draw (+1; §2.7). The `HOSTILE_CONFIDENCE` gate before `fromCap` keeps the undrawn index (the court's confidence in its whole strength, before it decides what to keep). Taken at the seed instead, after both gates had judged the undrawn army, a metropolis keeping nine `capable` riders would march 27 latent points below what its gate judged feasible — more than one band (floors 78/60/42/26); an optional argument to `seedDeploymentState` is no alternative, since the seed comes after the gates. `start` — and with it `maxStartStrength` and `currentEffectiveStrength`, the stateful strength `resolveSiegeVerdict` reads ("`const eff = Number.isFinite(stateful) ? … (stateful) : cap.offensive;`") — is the army without the riders the town kept. The per-tick `offensive` and `homeDefense` of `buildCapacityLookup` are NEVER touched (the kept riders are home, and the walls keep them); the draw is re-read only at a RE-DEPLOY (a new seed); the light-record migration (`ensureStatefulRecord`) seeds no draw — it is no decision ("the sizing DECISION is at the new-deploy site, not a migration"). `patrolDrawOf` = `DRAW_PER_BAND[band] × kept riders` (DRAFT: `thin` 1 · `modest` 2 · `capable` 3 latent points each; a city keeping six `modest` riders marches an army twelve points weaker — under one strength band). By construction the opener's feasibility gates and prefilter, the siege verdict and the attrition ledger read the drawn army (the gates through `drawn.cap`, the rest through the seed); allied relief reads the reliever's FIELD FORCE `homeDefense − siteDefense` (OD-PR-41's one composition), untouched by the draw. Dark ⇒ the copy is never made and the stamp is absent: byte-identical.

### 6.7 What patrols NEVER change
The stressor BIRTH gates (PR5; the landed rows are the standing watch) · the per-tick `offensive` and `homeDefense` (the draw is at deploy, §6.6) · `menaceCounterTerms` (the stamp carries the term, §6.2) · a sea hop's roll (no naval damper, §6.3) · the ramp's security relief · the embattlement level directly · `defenseLedger` (read-only; its importers include the frozen transit kernel) · the map's sway or its drawn danger (the map's law: "A patrol is never drawn lowering danger" stands for the MAP — the map draws the host's danger; the host's riders lower the host's danger because the host is the truth) · a plague's spread (no patrol slows a plague; a quarantine is the host's verb, §9.2(f)) · any named person's fate.

## §7 THE COUNTERFORCES (requirement 2) — every positive names its price

### 7.1 Wages and men
Riders cost: the PAY gate shrinks them with the town's prosperity (§3.1) — and, when W-COIN lights, coin through the treasury's `patrols` upkeep row (the slot); the POOL is the levy's own men, so riders kept on the roads while the army marches are a weaker field army (`patrolDraw`, §6.6). An UNPAID watch is few, thin, and takes bribes (§6.4's `BRIBE_KEEP`); an unpaid garrison is how a fort becomes a den (ST item 11(a): abandonment by want).

**The riders' purse is THE FISCAL SEAT's demand term when it lands.** The treasury's `patrols` upkeep row (the slot above, with ST's `sites` and CH's `retainers` rows) is read by THE FISCAL SEAT as a DEMAND term of the court's believed need (FS §3 — the need is the believed DEMAND, never the stock) once W-COIN's row lands; until then FS reads no rider cost and PR's PAY gate stands as written. FS prices no riders itself (the riders' purse is this row, OD-ST-5).

### 7.2 Loss — AMBUSH and the stronger body
A patrol or detachment that meets a body stronger than its band can be CUT DOWN (§11.2 `patrol_engages` → `cut_down`): a `losses` row (the count drops for a season — the recovery, §2.7), a DEED for the NAMED body that did it — RN's `DEED_SOURCES` row `patrol_clash` (§9.3), DOER THE BODY (`{ kind: 'body', id }`, keyed `g:<bodyId>` for a gang, its creed's `creed:<deityRef>` for a conclave; a beast and a nameless body are never doers, RN §2.1, so none), its leading paragon riding as `ledBy` (CH's `paragon.leads` at the act's tick — the one leader record) — ONE doer per deed and never also a `body_act` (RN §3.1: "ONE ACT, ONE DEED … a patrol's clash with a body is PR's `patrol_clash` … never a `body_act` beside them"); a detachment cut down INSIDE a LED hunt mints no `patrol_clash` (the mission's close is the one door — the body's `escaped` where the hunt fails), and its rumor ("Kell's riders fell at the ford"); a LED detachment cut down loses riders, never its leader (§5.5: no named fate); NO grievance input of its own — the commons' one registered input from PR is §7.5's, and a lost patrol's felt cost is the deed, the tale and the season without riders. No population is debited: a patrol's dead are below the census's grain — WC's people ledger owns headcounts when it lands (the slot), and the `losses` row is the fact. This is a recorded decision (macro over micro), vetoable: the alternative debits the town's population through the landed sink functions, a same-seed shift at a negligible size.

### 7.3 Fatigue — derived, never a stock
A detachment out more than `REST_BOUND` weeks (DRAFT 4) sends its riders `resting` for one week before they return to the round (a receipt); in wartime the riders' quality falls with the town's war exhaustion (`warExhaustion[id]`, the landed scar: `quality × (1 − FATIGUE_W × exhaustion01)`, `FATIGUE_W` 0.3). No fatigue stock exists, and no second decay law is minted (requirement 2's counterforce is read from the war's own scar and the detachment's own `weeksOut`). The brief's "a patrol's readiness wears on the beat and rests at home — a band", as a per-patrol band, would need per-patrol identity across weeks and a decaying stock; derived from `weeksOut` and the war's scar it needs neither.

### 7.4 False reports
A town's riders go where its belief sends them (§5.1), so a planted or staged report (IN's plant road; CH's provocateur) pulls riders off their round until the look finds nothing; the round's lowest stretches go unwatched meanwhile (§4.1 step 4 draws from the bottom of the round). The deceiver's road is IN's and CH's; PR prices the effect by construction.

### 7.5 Over-patrolling — the harassment the commons feel
THE TRIGGER IS THE FORK, never "`fielded` at `max`": when, in PEACE with nothing believed near, the court's own PEACE-POSTURE fork has held `peaceShare > PEACE_SHARE` for one season (§3.2: a wary court, a lawful-cruel seat) — `tick − forkSince ≥ INTERVAL_WEEKS.one_season` (`worldPulse/intervalWeeks.js :: INTERVAL_WEEKS`, "`one_season: 13,`"), where `forkSince` (§2.7) is the first week of the CURRENT unbroken run of such weeks, written by the stage at the run's first week, kept while the run holds and DELETED the week it breaks (war, a believed threat, the share at or below `PEACE_SHARE`, the town fielding none) — or the DM's standing `PATROL_ORDER` (`first` on `round`) has held one season (`tick − orders.setTick ≥ one_season`), the commons feel watched (the one carrier: CONFIRMED at master, `commonsVoiceKernel.js :: commonsGrievance01` is a pure per-call blend ("`return clamp01(CV.W_LEGIT * legitDeficit + CV.W_CORRUPT * … + CV.W_UNREST * …);`") and `strategicPosture.js :: courtRiskAppetiteOf` returns "`{kind, id, present, stock01, band, receipt}`", no since-tick, so the owner's "held for a season" is kept by ONE declared field, OD-PR-44, the owner's shape) — ONE registered input into the commons' ONE grievance home, `commonsVoiceKernel.js :: commonsGrievance01` ("THE ONE HOME of the commons grievance blend"), sized by the riders the fork or the order ACTUALLY ADDS: `W_HARASS × clamp01(excess / (max − ceil(max × PEACE_SHARE)))`, `excess = fielded − ceil(max × PEACE_SHARE)`, zero whenever `excess ≤ 0`; `W_HARASS` 0.10 (DRAFT, UNMEASURED; PLAUSIBLE until PR-1's car-0 measures the input's registration path in that kernel and its `commonsVoiceEnabled` gate) (the fork, never the count: with `fielded = ceil(max × peaceShare)` and the share ≥ 0.4, a one-rider town always stands "at `max`" — EXECUTED `cure/D-PR-r3-probe/arith.out` (2): 15 of 60 rows, 3 of them by rounding alone, the boldest court among them) (the excess gate: the bare fork would grieve a wary one- or two-rider town that fields EXACTLY the riders a median court of its size fields — `harass.out`: 6 of the fork's 36 rows — two identical pictures with two grievances; sized by the riders added, the input fires in 30 rows and only where the riders differ). **The alignment lens** (requirement 13): the fork itself carries it — a lawful-cruel seat's `harsh01` (`settlementAlignment`: `lawfulness01` × `malice01`) lifts its peace share toward `max` and the grievance follows (it keeps the watch and pays the grumbling gladly, since the fork never counts the grievance); a good seat adds nothing through `harsh01`, so only its own wariness (a low risk appetite) can lift its share and the grumbling with it — a good court with a bold or median appetite keeps the commons quiet; the DM's order pays the same price by the riders it adds, said on the card ("the commons grumble at riders on every road"). **The hunt among a body's friends** (THE BODIES §7.6, on the owner's 18:43 word "Go with your common's stance toward a boy"): the SAME one registered input takes a SECOND ARM — a week in which a `pursue` detachment rides in a town's own country after a body whose commons stance there is `support` (BD's `commonsStanceOf`) feeds `W_HARASS × clamp01(members / max)`, the hunters against the town's envelope — and the input reads the GREATER of its two arms, never their sum (one input, one weight); BD §7.6: "a HUNT there costs the court the commons' grievance (THE PATROLS' over-policing term `W_HARASS`, PR §7.5 — cite, never a second)" (vetoable: the alternative leaves such a hunt free and BD strikes its clause). The grievance input's exact host line is PLAUSIBLE, measured at PR-1's car-0.

### 7.6 The crown's riders over a lord's head — the grudge
Riders of the banner on a lord's roads OVER ITS HEAD are PL's PRECEDENCE FRICTION (PL §4.7 F.6 (b)): ONE `riders_quartered` ROW on that lord's own sworn edge, carrying ONE resentment patch (a grip term, so its deference falls, PL §4.9) — PL's own row, the OBLIGATION family, NEVER a weave incident (PL §15.9 no longer lists it) — in two arms: (i) THE SWEEP — the CROWN'S OWN riders PLACED on the lord's road halves UNASKED in a season in which no cover occasion stood for it (its own riders home, PL F.4), FOR A NEED THE LORD'S OWN RIDERS COULD HAVE MET, read at the season's end from the one SEASON TALLY's own-need count (`bannerCoverOf(…).swept > 0`, §2.6); a FELLOW MEMBER's riders riding by right of the banner, the crown's riders on its OWN need (its war roads), a threat the lord's own riders could not reach, and a ride the lord ASKED for (`patrol_joins`, §10.1 (d)) write nothing (PL: "never on a fellow member's riders riding by right (F.3) or on a threat beyond the lord's reach") — the crown's protection is the owner's "how we can express protection from the crown", never a grievance, at most one a season (episode `riders_quartered:<rootId>:<seasonStartTick>`); (ii) THE RIDE PAST — the crown's direct summons under `unbroken` that took a member's men past a lord it did not summon, or that refused or let the call lapse (PL §4.1 (ii); §10.3 (b)), at most one per lord per call; the resentment is the hold's own counterforce (PL's `D̄`). A season the banner's riders COVERED a member whose own riders went to the levy is PROTECTION (§10.3 (f): PL's `protection_kept`), never friction. A WEAK hold sends them home to the ladder (an ally's patrol on a member's road, §10.3 (d)); a BROKEN rung makes them a foreign unit (§10.3 (g)).

## §8 TRAVELLERS AND PATROLS — the encounter, the catch by kind, and planning on belief (the owner's fifth word; items 15, 16)

### 8.1 THE ONE ENCOUNTER LAW — `patrolEncounter`
For a named traveller on road half `h` (the half its LEG's `progress01` places it on against the gate's own fraction along the leg, §2.2) in week `t`, ONCE PER POWER whose riders stand on `h` that week (the placement):
- **MET** (truth; PR4): `watchOf(h, t, power) > 0` — that power's riders on the traveller's OWN half `h` that week, never the whole road; `by` names the power (the holder of the land, an ally, the crown). A traveller of the power's OWN town is never noticed by its own riders — they are its escort, not its watch.
- **NOTICED** (a keyed draw, stream `patrol:notice:<travellerKey>:<roadKey>:<power>:<t>`): `p = clamp(NOTICE_BASE × watch01 × wartime × stance × tower × (1 − cover), 0, NOTICE_CAP)` (every factor DRAFT, UNMEASURED; `NOTICE_BASE` 0.5, `NOTICE_CAP` 0.9):
  - `cover` — the traveller's own: a paragon's FAME AT THE PATROL'S TOWN read backwards (RN's `fameIn(worldState, actorRef, power, tick).scope` — fame as that town's BELIEF, never `fameOf`'s world-wide truth: `realm` −0.4 cover, `regional` −0.2; a famous enemy captain is recognised only where the riders' town has heard of him); NO cover-identity term exists (no landed or designed "merchant's face": ES's only covert skill is `covertCompetence01(home, npc)`, ES §3.2 — the home's criminal power and activity, damped by the operative's notoriety); a SPY's competence enters ONCE, in ES's own form, in the spy's row of §8.2 (never twice);
  - `stance` — the patrol's town toward the traveller's home, from the patrol's town's belief by the landed idiom: `b = belief(worldState, power, home)`; `label = b.source === 'belief' ? b.record.allianceLabel : b.source === 'truth' ? <the true label> : 'unknown'` (unknown reads as strangers): `hostile` 1.5 · strangers 1.0 · `allied` 0.5; a standing BAN on the traveller (the landed `ExclusionEdge`, widened to actor refs) × 2;
  - `wartime` — the patrol's town at war with anyone: × 1.3 (everyone is questioned);
  - `tower` — a held watchtower on the road (ST17): × 1.3;
  - quality is already inside `watch01` (band, pay, fatigue); a DETACHMENT on the half reads its summed band (a grouped detachment notices more).
- **AN ENVOY ANNOUNCES ITSELF:** for an envoy `noticed = met` (cover 0 means seen — no draw), and what follows is gated by the riders' town's rung toward the envoy's court (PS §6.5's default): `ally` → PASSED, no card ("a friend's envoy is not an engagement") · stranger → ONE card on PS's ENVOY ARM, default `escorted` · enemy → the same card, default `turned_back` (the arm's own line `stopped · escorted · turned_back`; PS's ledger module mints it, PR's stage calls the mint).
- **A BODY ON THE ROAD, ON A TRAIL, IN THE COUNTRY** (THE BODIES — a gang, a conclave, a beast; BD's route (1)) is a traveller ITS OWN STAGE moves (`bodies/bodyStage.js :: advanceBodies` calls this law after the riders are placed, one call per power whose riders stand at its place — BD §2.7). MET (truth): on a road half, as any traveller; on a hidden way `trail:<a|b>` (BD §5.2), ONLY where the power KNOWS the way and its EYES stand on one of the way's junction halves that week — `trailWatchOf` (§2.6), ZERO on a way it does not know; in a country or at a site, where a detachment stands there (a look, a hunt, a clearing). NOTICED: the draw above with BD's COVER term as `cover` — on a way its `cover01` from THE BODIES' `hiddenWayEdges`, the ONE read over the ONE ladder (master's hidden rung); on a road its size's visibility, BD's `ROAD_COVER_BY_MIGHT` (BD §9.1: "`thin` 0.5 · `modest` 0.3 · `capable` 0.15 · `strong`/`formidable` 0, DRAFT, UNMEASURED: a handful can pass for travellers, a war band cannot"); PLUS BD's commons term, +0.2 (DRAFT) wherever the body stands in the country of a town whose COMMONS SUPPORT it (BD §7.6: "its COVER in its town's country rises (one bounded term on BD's cover input to THE PATROLS' encounter law, DRAFT +0.2, PR §8.1)"; `commonsStanceOf` `support`) — the sum clamped to [0, 1] — and `stance` read from the power's COURT STANCE toward the body (BD's `courtStanceOf`, never a label of a pair the body has none of): `hunted` · `outlawed` · a beast 1.5 · `curbed` · `tolerated` 1.0 · `permitted` · `endowed` · `employed` · `harboured` 0 (the riders let it be — the owner's good conclave "not … pursued by patrols"). Met and noticed ⇒ for a pursued stance or a beast, `patrol_engages` (§11.2; THE HUNT's catch, §5.5); for `curbed` or `tolerated`, a `patrol_report` `crossing_seen` naming the body (the town's belief sharpens; no card); and a body that SEEKS a patrol it outmatches (the owner's word 14: "maybe sometimes patrols where they deem it appropriate"; BD14) opens the same `patrol_engages` as the aggressor, whatever the stance.
- **TWO BODIES** (BD's route (2); BD §9.1: "two bodies in the same place in the same week … meet through THE PATROLS' ONE encounter law WIDENED to body × body") — the law's SECOND WATCHER is a body: MET is co-location (truth: the same country, stretch, trail or site's attachment in the same week); `watch01` reads 1; the factors are BD's — the watcher's SEEK (a `feud` choice, a `hunt` stance toward a beast, hunger up; loot carried down) in place of `wartime × stance × tower`, the other body's cover as above; each side draws once (stream `bodies_meet:notice:<pairKey>:<watcherId>:<t>`, the digest's `pairKey` form); unnoticed by both they pass and nothing is minted; noticed by either, the card is BD's `bodies_meet` (BD's kind, BD's vocabulary). One law, two watchers; PR moves no body and hears no body-to-body meeting.
- **A PERSON OR A RUN ON A WAY** (CH §5's trail arm; L-BD 11b · 11c): a PARAGON whose leg takes a known way (CH OD-CH-42: "a known trail is taken at the landed person price `HIDDEN_PATH_SLOWDOWN` and met by `trailWatchOf`"), a covert envoy routed on its court's known ways (L-BD 11c; the landed `covert_envoy` franchise), a smuggler's run (§6.4) — any traveller master's hidden franchise admits — is MET there ONLY as a body is: where the power knows the way and its eyes stand on one of the way's junction halves (`trailWatchOf`, §2.6), ZERO on a way it does not know; NOTICED by the draw above, the way's `cover01` ADDED to the traveller's own cover (clamped to [0, 1]); and the catch by kind is §8.2's owner's, exactly as on the road (ES's `spy_at_target` at WHERE `road (a, b)` with the way named on the card, CH's gate words, the smuggling pipeline) — adding the way's cover to the person's own is a vetoable refinement.
- A patrol AWAY (`looking`, `converging`) is not on the half: it notices nothing.

ONE law, named at its seam; the consumers below call it at THEIR movers; PR moves no traveller.

### 8.2 WHAT A CATCH IS, BY KIND — each owner's ONE road, never a second law
| Traveller | Its mover (the consumer that calls §8.1) | Noticed ⇒ | Owner |
|---|---|---|---|
| a SPY on a covert errand (ES) | ES's in-transit errand pass | ONE ROAD EXPOSURE, the patrolled road as ES's third exposure class: PR's notice IS the road's catch roll — for a spy `p = clamp(NOTICE_BASE × watch01 × wartime × stance × tower × (1 − SHELTER_W · covertCompetence01(home, npc)), 0, CATCH_CAP)`, ES's competence entering ONCE in ES's own form (`SHELTER_W`, `CATCH_CAP`) and the stance once (PR's, by belief); ES's `catch01` is NOT run on the road (its terms — `orderFactor`, `porosity`, `underways`, `legStack`, `dwellRamp` — are the hostile TOWN's and have no road meaning). Noticed = `caught`: the outcome and the taint cascade are ES's — the landed `spy_at_target` kind with WHERE `road` (`caught · uncaught`; ruled, OD-PR-15). ES §3.3's J-ES-1 ("Two exposure classes, BY CONSTRUCTION of the host … no bandits, no sea lanes, no ambient risk"; "an exposure event, not a patrol event") gains an amendment bracket for the owner's fifth word ("spies crossing paths with a patrol unit have an appropratie chance of getting caught") — the fold's to write | ES |
| an ENVOY in hostile land (the errand spine) | the errand spine's in-transit pass | STOPPED only as a ROAD HEARING — THE ENVOY ARM of PS's `passage_at_the_border` (PS §6.5), WHERE `road{a, b}`, with its own closed line `stopped · escorted · turned_back` and no mover line (an envoy is never a passage mover, PS1), its default by the rung (ally: no card · stranger `escorted` · enemy `turned_back`) — NEVER `envoy_intercepted` / `envoy_held`: those are the ARMY interception's (`envoyEncounter.js`: "Exact node equality is the whole collision law"; `foreignGuestHold.js :: normalizeForeignGuestHold` drops any hold without an `encounterId`), and a patrol never HOLDS an envoy: ES-2b — OPENED by delegation, no longer the owner's slot; dark until ES's custody wave lands — admits a held person only in the shapes as written (ES-2b's row: a body's ransom captive, BD §9.10 · "a person caught on a way", this section's trail arm, the paragon row below · a held paragon, CH §12.4 — "and nothing wider"), and an envoy held by a patrol is not among them (PS §6.5; a new custody shape would be the owner's row); PS's ledger module is the card's one writer and PR's stage calls its mint; the engine never harms an envoy; the `stopped` envoy's week RIDES THE OPPORTUNITY's ONE errand-family verb `detourEnvoyErrand`'s `delay` contract (same stops, later legs), applied at the NODE THE ENVOY NEXT REACHES (every contract re-plans only from an `arrived` cursor); ONE shared verb, minted by whichever of OP-1 and PR's stop wave lands first, the other registering (PS §6.5's S-e) | PS |
| an ENEMY PARAGON on its leg (CH) | THE ONE PERSON STAGE (CH's weekly pass) | recognised and TURNED BACK: CH's town's-gate words `refused` (`person_at_the_gate`'s vocabulary) at the road — on a known way met ONLY by the trail arm (§8.1); SEIZED only when custody lands — ES-2b, OPENED by delegation: LIVE DESIGN dispatching in its own wave, this row among its admitted shapes (ES-2b's row: "a person caught on a way (`DESIGN_FP_ARCH_PR.md` §8.2's trail arm)", "a held PARAGON (`DESIGN_FP_ARCH_CH.md` §12.4)"); the wave order stands — ES's custody wave first, then BD-4's ransom race and CH's road arm, with which this arm builds; until that wave lands NO engine custody and the DM's hand `heldAt` (CH §12.4) is the only hold; the hold row is ES-2b's one custody road and the verdict CH's road arm's — PR adds no word and no record (nothing widened) | CH |
| a liaison's run, a villain's runner (CH's network) | the same | CH's gate words; the corruption web's exposure (`corruptionWeb.js :: applyForeignExposureBlowback`, source `covert_hire_exposed`) when the runner carries a clandestine hire | CH |
| the PARTY (PT) | PT's journey (the DM's toggle moving the party along a route) | PT's `party_gate` WIDENED to WHERE `road (a, b)`, fired by a patrolled traversal of the DM's own move — CH's words unchanged (`admitted · refused · admitted_under_watch · struck`), no new kind (ruled, OD-PR-14; PT's row and PR's cite) — its default "nothing has happened to the party yet"; a court's `party_ambush` laid on the road sends a DETACHMENT of its riders as PT's WATCH (PR19); a `party_warrant` stands at the court's town, never on a road (PT's row) | PT |
| a caravan (WY / EC-18) | WY-6's caravan encounter | WY-6's `caravan_seized` (`seized · ransomed · passed`) at the patrol's stance — a hostile patrol may seize; an escorted caravan's seizure odds fall (§10.4) — recorded by ONE fold §3.1 WY INBOX row (WY-6 calls `patrolEncounter`; a patrol POWER as the seizer beside the body seizer; §9.1 (c)'s turned-back caravans; `escortedOn`'s fall); DARK until WY carries it | WY |
