## §14 THE WAVES (every wave: car-0 · leaves · hosts · FIRST-VISIBLE · TIER · DEGRADED ARM · EDITOR (a forks · b seals and predicates · c the counterparty · d the tick and states · e the record) · Alignment · `causedBy`)

PR is a coupling CARRIER across **WAR · TRADE · INFO · POPULATIONS**; its prefix `PR` is chartered with its first coupling-registry row (requirement 21(a)/(c)). HBF rows minted, each in its wave's commit (L10(a)): `patrolEngagesContest`, `patrolNotice`, `patrolLook`. Chain **C16 THE PATROLS** sits AFTER C15 THE SITES, ADJACENT — C14 · C15 · C16 share the border gate and the fort (OD-PR-1); after C0 · C0.5 · C1 — and, by a vetoable ruling under the owner's "i leave to your recommendation", BEHIND the map's M-W2 and the war layer's lighting ("MOVE C14 THE PASSAGE and C16 THE PATROLS behind the map's M-W2 and the war layer's lighting (… they do nothing in the default preset)"), C14 still beside it; the fold's §6 states the order; it reads both, and PS's `witnessedCrossing` waits on it; its FIRST FELT CHAIN is PR-0 → PR-1 (riders on the roads; the slow and fast halves). Its map prerequisites, for the fold's §6: H13 / H23 on M-W5's sway-and-patrols step plus H25 for the window rows — never H16.

**PR-0 — THE RIDERS AND THE ROUND (`patrolsEnabled`).**
- **Car-0 (MEASUREMENTS before any PR line):** (i) the stage host — `calamityKernel.js :: advanceCalamity` (461) with MN's and ST's leaves beside PR's (+3); MN-0's four STOP conditions inherited; ⛔ STOP at build if no room — PR's stage follows MN-0/ST-0's host; (ii) the census of every PR token on the then-current master (`patrol`, `on_round`, `driven_off`, `cut_down`, `will_see`, the cadence words); (iii) the census population's distribution WITHIN each tier across the soak corpus (the pool ratio's real spread; `cure/D-PR-r2-probe/pool-r2.out` is the arithmetic, the corpus the measure); (iv) the `MOVEMENT_SITES` row for `patrols/patrolMove.js`; (v) the placement digest's size on the soak corpus (rows × halves; the drop-when-empty pin).
- **Leaves:** `patrols/patrolLedger.js` (the one writer; the four maps' normalizer; drop-when-empty; the `losses` recovery and horizon; FNV ids), `patrols/envelope.js` (`patrolEnvelopeOf`, §3), `patrols/round.js` (`roundOf`, `onStretchThisWeek`, `watchOn`, `watchOf`, `coverageOf`, `patrolPlacementOf`), `patrols/patrolMove.js` (the leg price — `namedPersonLegTicks`), `patrols/patrolStage.js :: advancePatrols` (§4.1 steps 1, 2, 5), `patrols/witness.js` (`witnessedCrossing`). **Host edits:** `calamityKernel.js` (+3); PS's `passageBorderLeaf.js` imports `witnessedCrossing` instead of its `true` stub (PS's commit or PR's, whichever lands second).
- **FIRST-VISIBLE:** the dossier's defence page the advance the flag lights — "Kell keeps riders on the north road and the ford road every week" (surface: the dossier; the map's lens behind its patrol toggle once it draws the window's rows, §13); scrutiny: the four gates in words ("a town; its people enough for four patrols; its purse meagre; its watch firm").
- **TIER:** every preset; premium; aspatial-safe (graph hops); the omniscient arm identical (no belief involved in the round) · **DEGRADED ARM:** no canon ⇒ stretches are graph edges and the danger arm (PR-1) is dark; no `hearingWeekEnabled` ⇒ irrelevant here (no hearing in PR-0).
- **EDITOR:** (a) forks — none (a roving half's slot and its rover's base are `fnv1a32` keys of the half and the power, §2.3, registered in HBF as `patrolPhase` with no draw); (b) predicates `patrolsActive`, `onStretchThisWeek`; (c) the counterparty — none; (d) the tick — weekly; the placement written once for the next tick and read by every consumer (PR4); no institution or person write; (e) the record — `spatialLedgers.patrols` (§2.7) is worldState-side lived history outside the record register and `rederive`; `PATROL_COMMANDS · PATROL_STATES · PATROL_CADENCES · DETACHMENT_AIMS` are save-data contract.
- **Alignment (requirement 13):** none at PR-0 — the envelope reads tier, people, purse and order; alignment enters at PR-1 (§7.5) and PR-2 (the court's forks).
- **`causedBy`:** the envelope's receipt names its four gates' reads; a stretch's cadence names the round's priority reasons.

**PR-1 — THE EFFECTS (`patrolEffectsEnabled`).**
- **Car-0:** measure `banditryLoss`'s and `smuggleSuccessChance`'s callers (exactly `commodityFlow.js:651`, `supplyShipments.js:372`, `commodityFlow.js:602` at master — any new caller gets `fireLevel` / `watch01` or is listed as reading none); measure ST's one additive slot leaf (`siteTermAt`) for a second row (`criminal_corridor`) — ⛔ STOP at build if ST-1 has not landed AND `stressorDynamics.js` cannot take ≤ 2 lines (then the counter term waits on ST-1; it NEVER rides `menaceCounterTerms` — MN's slot (2) is filled through the stamp, and MN's own fallback reads `siteTermAt` directly); the deploy-draw host — `warDeployment.js :: evaluateWarLayer` (685), +2, the drawn capacity right after `fromCap` and BEFORE both feasibility gates and the target loop, and the stamp's ONE conditional line beside the seed (`seedDeploymentState`, `warArmyRecord.js` 72 eff, is not edited: the seed comes after the gates, and the stamp rides the record the way `joinLedger` and `casusReasons` do); measure whether THE SHARED PRESENCE COMPOSER stands — if not, MN's OWNED unit is cut with PR-1 (its manifest row and its text MN's, OD-PR-43); measure `commonsGrievance01`'s input registration path (§7.5's PLAUSIBLE); measure the correlation of `buildStrengthLookup`'s index with `offensive` (the prefilter's `drawShare01`, §6.6, PLAUSIBLE); measure the four-week ring and the joint floor (§6.3) against the soak corpus; PR-1's `keepRiders` term (§9.1 (e)) is ONE registered claimant row on WAR CIRCULATION's owned `WC-car-0 THE CHOOSER'S SIGNAL CAR` (`settlementStrategy.js`, leaf-first, cut before EC-17 as the first claimant's prerequisite — fold §3.1.23): PR-1's term row lands only after that car, and PR-1 adds no line of its own to `settlementStrategy.js`.
- **Leaves:** `patrols/patrolEffects.js` (`patrolSubtraction`, `patrolCounterTerm`, `patrolDrawOf`, `patrolDrawnCap`, the `BRIBE_KEEP` table); `patrols/patrolDrawStamp.js` (`normalizePatrolDrawStamp`); and — ONLY if C16 is the first of C14 · C15 · C16 · C10 to be cut — MN's OWNED composer unit cut with it, `spatial/presenceComposer.js :: presenceTerms` (its neutral home, MN §3.3; its text and its manifest row MN's, OD-PR-43; never a PR re-implementation). PR's subtraction is ONE row of that module's closed registration list, registered if the car stands. **Host edits:** `embattlement.js` (+2: `banditryLoss`'s `fireLevel`; +≤2 more ONLY where MN's composer unit is cut with PR-1 — the composer's call before `stepEmbattlement`, MN §4.2's seam); `commodityFlow.js` (+2: `fireLevel` and the gate town's `watch01`); `supplyShipments.js` (+1); `smuggle.js` (+2); `warDeployment.js :: evaluateWarLayer` (685, +2: the drawn capacity right after `fromCap`, read by both feasibility gates, the prefilter and the seed; the stamp's conditional line beside the seed, §6.6); `worldState.js :: normalizeDeployments` (351, +1: the stamp's fail-closed block through `patrols/patrolDrawStamp.js :: normalizePatrolDrawStamp`, §16.1); ST's `siteEffects.js` (+1 row: the corridor; the raider row is ST's slot); `commonsVoiceKernel.js` (+≤2, PLAUSIBLE).
- **FIRST-VISIBLE:** the week riders stand on an embattled stretch a caravan crosses — the trade receipt "The caravan reached Kell untouched; Kell's riders were on the hill road" (scrutiny: the fast half's reason) · the season the slow half takes a country raided by dens or roaming brigands below the latch's exit (never while a menace raids it, §6.1) — the dossier's threat page "Kell's riders have the roads again" · a smuggling catch — "Kell's riders confiscated Varn cloth run past the embargo".
- **TIER:** the danger arm (§6.1) canonized only (the embattlement marker); the counter term in every stressor preset; the fast and smuggling halves under `commodityFlowEnabled` (the commerce physics); the draw under `warLayerEnabled`; premium · **DEGRADED ARM:** aspatial — the counter term and the draw only.
- **EDITOR:** (a) forks — none new (the leans ride the hosts' own keyed draws); (b) the predicates `watched`, `patrolEffectsActive`; (c) real settlements only (`watchOn` / `watchOf` name powers that exist); (d) the hosts' ticks; (e) the deployment record's conditional `patrolDraw` stamp (§2.7); every lean otherwise derived from the placement.
- **Alignment:** §7.5's harassment input reads the seat's `settlementAlignment` (a lawful-cruel seat keeps riders out and pays the grievance gladly).
- **`causedBy`:** each lean's receipt names the stretch, the power and the week (the slow half the four-week window).

**PR-2 — REACT, GROUP, DISPERSE; LOOKS; REPORTS; THE CLASH (`patrolsEnabled`).**
- **Car-0:** measure `migrationRumors.js :: rumorCarrierParams` (83) for the report lane (+≈6 via `patrols/patrolReports.js`); measure the investigable lists (§9.2(a)) against the composer's catalog (`eventComposer/buildEvent.js`'s types) and cut any absent; NAME THE LANDED CARRIERS: the authored-event domain read (`events/drainQueuedEvents.js :: drainQueuedEvents`' tick-stamped `eventLog` entries, or its `authoredEvent` threaded by the advance, §9.2 (a)) and the refused-join patch's writer (`relationshipEvolution.js :: applyRelationshipPatch`, cause `patrol_join_refused`, §10.1); measure MN's `menace_met` trigger list for the detachment producer row (MN's commit); census ST's `site_cleared` resolver for a detachment as the force; confirm THE BODIES' `believedBodies`, `believedBodyOf`, `courtStanceOf`, `commonsStanceOf`, `bodyStrength`, `ROAD_COVER_BY_MIGHT` and `BODY_HABITS` reads, its one known set `knownTrailsOf`, its ladder read `hiddenWayEdges` and its one writer `bodies/hiddenWays.js` (for `by_catch`, `by_pursuit` and the `watched` mark) (BD-1, BD-5) — ⛔ STOP at build for THE HUNT if they have not landed (then §5.1's body row reads nothing and no hunt forms; everything else in PR-2 stands); measure the riders' WAY ADMISSION: PR's own leaf read of `knownTrailsOf(…).get(trailKey)` on a way-mode hop (`routeEdgeId(a, b, 'way')`) inside the holder's land — `routeNetworkConsumers.js` UNTOUCHED (no token, no pin re-recorded) and whether PR's leg leaf carries the hidden kind at `gradeLegMultiplier('hidden')` (H3's `planWanderLeg` the precedent) — ⛔ STOP at build for the riders' ways if it cannot (then every detachment keeps to the roads and the junctions; the trail arm and gleaning stand).
- **Leaves:** `patrols/threats.js` (`believedThreatsIn`), `patrols/detachments.js` (§5.2–5.4), `patrols/look.js` (§9.2), `patrols/patrolReports.js` (§9.3), `patrols/patrolHearings.js` (`patrol_engages`'s resolver, registered with SP-9), `patrols/hunt.js` (`predictedPlaceOf`, THE HUNT's pursuit, cold trail and led arm, the riders' hidden franchise and THE GLEANING ROLL — §5.5). **Host edits:** NONE on `routeNetworkConsumers.js` (no `patrol` token joins `TRAVELLER_KINDS` or `HIDDEN_PATH_KINDS` and no pin is re-recorded — OD-PR-47; the riders' way admission is PR's own leaf read of `knownTrailsOf`); `migrationRumors.js` (+≈6); SP-9's registry (`patrol_engages`); ST's `site_cleared` producer list (ST's commit); MN's `menace_met` producer row (MN's commit); CH's `callBoard` source row (CH's commit).
- **FIRST-VISIBLE:** the week a look reports — "Kell's riders went up to the hills and found nothing but old fires" / "Kell's riders report a den at the old fort" (the zero-click suggestion beside it); the week riders ride together — "Kell's riders rode out together against the band at the old fort"; a clash card the week it climaxes.
- **TIER:** every preset; reports TRAVEL under infoMode `full` (a news line in the news-only presets; a chronicle line under the omniscient arm); `hearingWeekEnabled` for the pause; premium · **DEGRADED ARM:** no rumor road ⇒ reports are chronicle lines and false reports do not exist; no hearing week ⇒ clashes resolve on their defaults in the tick.
- **EDITOR:** (a) forks — `patrolEngagesContest` (`rng.fork('patrol:engages:<detachmentId>:<tick>')`), `patrolLook` (`rng.fork('patrol:look:<settlementId>:<placeKey>:<tick>')`); (b) the seal of `patrol_engages` read FIRST by its resolver; predicates `threatBelieved`, `groupReaches`; (c) the counterparty — a real settlement, a corps, or a body ref `{ kind: 'body', id }` (never a court); (d) weekly — a hunt's prediction weekly, its cold trail read from the newest sighting's tick; (e) `detachments` (with `pursuedRef`, `leaderRef`) and `losses` (§2.7).
- **Alignment:** the court's response fork reads its risk posture (`strategicPosture.js`, requirement 10) — a lawful court answers a den on its road, a chaotic one lets it lie unless pressed; a martial court strikes first in a stale-belief meeting.
- **`causedBy`:** a detachment names the believed threat's source (the report, the rumor, the witnessed crossing); a dispersal names its finishing verdict or report; a look names its trigger (the stressor or the authored event).

**PR-3 — THE BORDER HAND, THE TRAVELLERS AND THE SCREEN (with PS; `patrolsEnabled`).**
- **Car-0:** confirm PS's car-0 has landed (the transit kernel's extraction, `TRANSIT_LEAVES`, and THE ONE HOLD HELPER with its FREEZE mode) — ⛔ STOP at build for the SCREEN if PS's S-a fired (the screen then does not exist; everything else in PR-3 stands); measure each traveller mover's seam for §8.1's call (ES's in-transit pass, the errand spine's pass, THE ONE PERSON STAGE, WY-6's encounter, THE BODIES' stage — its body arm and its body-by-body arm) — each in its OWN owner's commit.
- **Leaves:** `patrols/encounter.js` (`patrolEncounter` with its two watchers and its body arm, `believedWatch` with its trail keys and believed phase, `trailWatchOf`, `escortedOn`), `patrols/patrolScreenLeaf.js` (the `TRANSIT_LEAVES` registrant), `patrols/escort.js`. **Host edits:** `transitLeafStage.js :: TRANSIT_LEAVES` (+1 registration row); SP-9's registry (`patrol_engages` gains the screen's climax); each consumer's own commit (ES, the errand spine, CH, WY; PT's `party_gate` road arm; PS's envoy arm and patrol mover).
- **FIRST-VISIBLE:** the first week of a war — "Kell's riders came in from the north road with word of Merewick's host at the ford" (the war's first sign in Kell, requirement 16); the screen — "Kell's riders fell at the ford; the levy had its week"; a spy caught on the road — "Ashford's riders took a Kell spy on the river road"; an envoy stopped — "Ashford's riders turned back Kell's envoy on the hill road".
- **TIER:** the war arms under `warLayerEnabled`; the spy under `espionageEnabled`, the envoy under `errandSpineEnabled`, the paragon under `npcConsequencesEnabled` (OD-FC-39's lighting unit, dramatic and full); premium · **DEGRADED ARM:** no transit leaf ⇒ no screen; no traveller substrate ⇒ the encounter law has no consumer and does nothing.
- **EDITOR:** (a) forks — `patrolNotice` (`rng.fork('patrol:notice:<travellerKey>:<roadKey>:<power>:<tick>')` — §8.1's stream, the power included); (b) predicates `met`, `noticed`; the screen's hold — the one helper's FREEZE mode, bounded by PS9's cap where THE PASSAGE is lit and by Bound 1 alone where it is dark — and its one week at both advance lengths pinned (R18); (c) the traveller's home is a real settlement; (d) the movers' ticks; (e) nothing stored by PR (the catches are the owners' records).
- **Alignment:** a stance read is BELIEF (`belief(holder, home).allianceLabel`); the screen's `screened`/`withdrew` fork reads the court's posture.
- **`causedBy`:** a catch names the encounter (stretch, power, week); a screen names the army's transit record and the report that set the detachment.

**PR-4 — THE KINGDOM, ALLIES AND THE DM'S ORDER (`patrolsEnabled`).**
- **Car-0:** measure `realmManifest.js` (474) and `realmVerbExecution.js` (663) for `PATROL_ORDER` (leaf-first, ≤ 2 + ≤ 3 host lines); confirm PL's `polityOf` (its `path`), `polityHoldOf`, `crownReachAt` and `swornLinkSuspended` reads (THE BANNER's inputs) and PL-5's `call_in_answer` input line for riders (no PR kind, no fealty row of PR's); confirm PL's shape-3 kind `riders_quartered` (a ROW with its one resentment patch, PL §4.7 F.6 (b)) and PL's reads of `bannerCoverOf`'s SEASON TALLY for its protection and friction rows (PL's commit); confirm the stage's season tally `weeksCovered` round-trips through the ledger's normalizer (§16.1); confirm PS's jurisdiction read answers same-kingdom riders by right (PS's commit).
- **Leaves:** `patrols/banner.js` (THE BANNER, §10.3: the net's composition over PL's BANNER STACK (read through `swornLinkSuspended`, PL §5.3's GUARDED set) inside the stage's step 5 — reading PS's `passageStandingOf(…).banner` for each half's right — and `bannerCoverOf`; never a separate `crownRiders.js`), `patrols/allies.js` (§10.1, `patrol_joins`), `patrols/patrolVerbs.js` + `patrolVerbApply.js`. **Host edits:** `realmManifest.js` (+≤2), `realmVerbExecution.js` (+≤3), SP-9's registry (`patrol_joins`).
- **FIRST-VISIBLE:** "The crown's riders keep the Tolle hills since Kell's riders went to the war" (§10.3 (i): the season the net covers a member whose riders went to the levy); "Merewick's riders were turned back at the Kell ford, where they rode freely last season" (§10.3 (g): the first sign of a broken rung); "Tolle's riders rode with Ashford's against the band in the hills"; the DM's order receipt the same advance.
- **TIER:** THE BANNER (the crown's riders, the right, the net) where `polityHoldEnabled` is lit, and the riders' line on PL-5's summons where that summons lights; PL's protection rows where `polityObligationsEnabled` is; allies everywhere `patrolsEnabled` is; aspatial-safe (the stack and the right are graph reads; the net's shared halves are the graph's edges); premium · **DEGRADED ARM:** no polity ⇒ every town a stack of one, no crown's riders and no net; the join's default reads the landed pair state in every arm.
- **EDITOR:** (a) forks — the joins' and the summons' defaults are court forks on landed reads (no new draw); (b) `patrol_joins`' seal; (c) the asker, the ally, the member and the root are real settlements; (d) weekly; (e) `orders` (§2.7); `PATROL_ORDER` round-trip pinned through the edits-delta idiom (requirement 14).
- **Alignment:** a lawful member answers PL's summons with its riders more readily (PL's deference fork); a chaotic one answers `half_answered`, keeping them.
- **`causedBy`:** a join names the threat and the ask; a member's riders on the war's roads name PL's summons.

**PR-5 — THE MEASURE.**
- **What it measures:** every DRAFT constant of §17 on the soak corpus (the envelope's distribution by tier, the cadence bucketing, the presence subtraction's share of latch exits, the fast half's incident reduction, the smuggling catch rate by pay band, the draw's share of siege verdicts moved, the screen's binding rate against PS9's cap, the notice rates by traveller kind); THE JOINT FLOOR (§6.3: per watched embattled hop, the expected loss against the same hop unwatched, ≥ `JOINT_FLOOR`); THE ONE-PLACEMENT PIN (R30); THE DOUBLE-COUNT PROBE (§6.2: the correlation of `patrolCounterTerm` with the landed `defense_readiness` and `institution defense` terms — ρ > 0.7 narrows the reference); the experience read (requirement 19): over a seed family, the share of lit worlds in which each felt sentence of §18 fires at least once; THE LEGIBILITY PIN: every pushed patrol line resolves to ≥ 1 hop in the cause walk; the push envelope (patrol lines never pushed alone, PR18).
- **FIRST-VISIBLE:** none of its own · **TIER:** the soak harness · **EDITOR:** none · **Alignment:** the harassment and the summons rates per alignment quadrant reported · **`causedBy`:** the measurement's receipts cite the runs.

## §15 PATROLS CONSIDERED — the coverage table and the token register

### 15.1 THE COVERAGE TABLE — one row per FP program (each existing volume carries ONE "patrols considered" line citing its row)
| Program | How it considers patrols |
|---|---|
| SP (the spine) | SP-9's docket hosts PR's two kinds (`registerEngagementKind`); the actor convention unchanged (riders are a settlement's) |
| CW (C0.5 THE CAUSE WALK) | every PR receipt carries `causedBy`; the walk reaches a catch, a screen, a look and a lean with no wiring |
| GR (grammar) | no PR term; a peace's `site_cession` or a treaty moves the round through the sites and the ladder by construction |
| OP (THE OPPORTUNITY) | the stopped envoy's week rides OP's `detourEnvoyErrand` `delay` contract (§8.2's envoy row); a rescuer's exert moves no `patrol_will` (§3.1); OP's operative on a road is a traveller of the ONE encounter law through ES's arm (`spy_at_target`'s road arm) — no new PR row |
| MG (THE MOVEMENT GRAMMAR) | the look and the screen are UNIT-SIGHTING producers (§9.1 (b), §9.2 (b)); a patrol's movement verbs are NAMED from PR's own aims, never rolled (MG §3.6); the screen holds the army only, MG carrying it to the wings; a riding gang reads one band wider in `believedWatch`; the riders' leg takes WY's `horse` ratio by WY's ruling |
| FS (THE FISCAL SEAT) | the treasury's `patrols` upkeep row is FS's demand term when W-COIN's row lands (§7.1); FS prices no riders |
| IN (information) | reports are rumor feed entries at hop 0 (§9.3); IN's plant road makes false reports (§7.4); a road can be made to LOOK watched or empty (§8.3) |
| ES (espionage) | the patrolled road is ES's THIRD exposure class — ONE roll, PR's notice, with ES's competence once in ES's own form and ES's outcome (`spy_at_target`, WHERE `road`) (§8.2); J-ES-1's amendment bracket is the fold's (OD-PR-15); ES's product row is a `believedWatch` producer, and mission planning reads it |
| TR / EC (trade, economy) | the fast half at `banditryLoss` (per hop, the roll only); the gate town's own riders at `smuggle.js`'s worst gate; EC-6's stance makes goods contraband; EC-18's open road and the caravans time departures by `believedWatch`; `route_wardenship` stays EC-6's stance term (OD-FC-2), never a patrol |
| WF (faith) | none of its own — a creed's people act through the town's own numbers; a creed's moving flock is THE BODIES' conclave, whom the riders let be, watch or hunt by the court's stance (§5.5; BD §7.1) |
| POP (populations) | columns escorted away from a front (§10.4) — their road deaths fall with `escortedOn`, recorded by a fold §3.1 POP INBOX row; road deaths read the danger ledger (by construction); the harassment input to the commons' ONE grievance home (§7.5) |
| INT (the interior) | NOT APPLICABLE — factions read the town's own numbers; the will gate reads the town's order (`defenseLedger.internal`), never a faction |
| WY (wayfare) | caravans' `caravan_seized` odds fall with an escort (`escortedOn`), WY-6 calls `patrolEncounter` and a patrol power may seize — ONE fold §3.1 WY INBOX row records all three; the host's patrol rows go out through the window (§13), never through H16; a patrol's map piece is a presence, never an intention |
| HB (habit) | the round's habit term (§2.2): HB's ONE curve on the settlement actor under the landed class `route_menaced`, the stretch key its action token — HB's car-0 admits the token or the term stays dark (no thirteenth class); the token and the report credit recorded by a fold §3.1 HB INBOX row; the round's cadence is a habit a body learns through belief (§2.3), and a hunt's leader's axes and a body's own habits are HB's person and body grains (§5.5) — PR mints no learning law |
| WC (war circulation) | the kept riders leave the army's SEED at deploy (the draw, §6.6) — never the per-tick capacity; a corps (CH's unit mark) crosses as a military unit (§8.2); WC-12's located brigands ARE THE BODIES' gangs, met on the road in `patrol_engages` by PR's band contest — WC-12's `EMBATTLED_RESOLUTIONS` close a brigand SIEGE, never a road fight (§5.3); WC-1 credits allied joins; WC-5's call-ins go out in the screen's week; WC's people ledger is the slot for riders' headcounts |
| EP (the advance epoch) | long advances run the round and every hearing on defaults; nothing queues |
| PL (the polity) | THE BANNER (§10.3): a patrol's loyalty stack is PL's `polityOf(…).path`, derived; the liege's share of a member's riders is PL's ONE share, `POLITY_LIEGE_SHARE[band]` toward the member's IMMEDIATE liege (PL §4.7 F.2, PL's row; `crownReachAt`'s reach is the crown's voice in a sub-vassal's decisions, never a call on its men); the NET's call reads the crown's OWN hold (`polityHoldOf(…, rootId, rootId)`, PL §3.1 (e)) while a crossing's right is PS's read of the HOLDER's belief (PS §5.5); PL's `swornLinkSuspended`, PL §6's feuds and a break break the rung; the call reaches riders rung by rung through PL's summons under every hold below `unbroken` and DIRECTLY under `unbroken`, never skipping a lord's ROW (the mid-lord's credit or precedence friction, PL §4.1 (ii); §10.3 (b)); a member lends `max(1, ⌊n × s × hold01⌋)` above the home's minimum, a sub-vassal through its liege's line (PL §4.7 F.2); PL's PROTECTION rows read `bannerCoverOf`'s SEASON TALLY (PL §4.7 F.4) and its SERVICE row credits lent riders (PL §4.7 F.6) — PL's writer, PR requests no row; the swept lord's grudge is PL's `riders_quartered` ROW with its one resentment patch (§7.6; PL §4.7 F.6 (b)); the crown's call on a member's riders is PL-5's `call_in_answer` (`half_answered` keeps them home) — no PR kind; feuds turn riders hostile (§10.1(f)); the crown's embargo makes goods contraband (`confiscation`); the `patrol:` episode prefix PL registers prices a PATROL's passage (PS's request), never a PR fealty row; PL §15.12's "the CROWN'S ROAD WARDENS" reads "the crown's riders" in PL's own text |
| MN (the menace) | MN §5.5's slot (2) is filled THROUGH THE STAMP (PR's term enters ST's slot once); riders subtract inside MN §3.3's ONE composition — THE SHARED PRESENCE COMPOSER, MN's OWNED unit, cut with whichever chain is cut first (OD-PR-43) — on the suppressible part only; a raiding menace keeps an embattled town embattled, its level easing (MN M53); a grouped detachment meets a menace through MN's `menace_met` (a producer row; `army_beaten` reads "an army or a detachment"); riders report a menace (the court picture) |
| RN (renown) | catches, clashes, clearings and screens are candidate engine-source deed rows (`patrol_catch`, `patrol_clash`) — ONE ACT, ONE DEED: an unled clash the riders' town's TALE, a patrol cut down by a named body the BODY's deed (its RN key `g:<bodyId>`, its creed's for a conclave; its leading paragon `ledBy`), a LED hunt's success CH's `hunt_won` at the mission's close and never a `patrol_clash`; a paragon's fame AT THE PATROL'S TOWN (`fameIn`) cuts its cover (§8.1); the join's default reads the landed pair state, never RN's court regard (RN's refused pairs) |
| CH (champions) | a warden leads riders through `paragonLean(…, 'wildland', 'patrol_rider_quality')`, a magistrate raises the will through CH's `patrol_will` seam (§10.5); a warden HOLDING the public-security office knows the hidden ways of its land (L-BD 41, §5.5; never a warden merely retained); a paragon on a known way is met only by the trail arm (§8.1); a paragon may LEAD a hunt by CH's purpose `hunt_body`, moving by its own mover on PR's one prediction (`predictedPlaceOf`), and its failure is CH's `paragon_disgraced` (L-BD 40); a hostile paragon is turned back by CH's gate words; a villain's runners carry covert cover and buy unpaid riders; a report feeds the call board |
| PT (the party) | the party meets riders on the road only through PT's `party_gate` at WHERE `road` (ruled, OD-PR-14); PT's WATCH on the road (`party_ambush`; a `party_warrant` stands at the court's town) is a detachment of the court's riders (PR19); PT consumes no `believedWatch` (the DM sees all); a look may find the party's traces (PT §11's visibility); a body never acts toward the party (BD15) and a party meeting a body is PT's road arm, never a PR card |
| PS (the passage) | PR OWNS `witnessedCrossing` (the holder's riders on its half of the gate's road); PS's escort is a detachment; a patrol crossing a border is PS's mover (`PASSAGE_MOVER_KINDS` holds `patrol` and `corps`) and an envoy the riders stop is heard on PS's envoy arm (§6.5); the screen is the third registrant in PS §4.1's ONE order and is held by PS's ONE helper in its FREEZE mode, inside PS9's one cap where PS is lit; PS's unit key (the deployment id) holds across a screened week; PS's enemy-column feeder reaches the destination's own land (§9.1 (c)); THE BANNER: PS's jurisdiction read answers same-kingdom riders BY RIGHT where the HOLDER believes the crown's hold strong (a `banner` permit, no spend, one card defaulting `granted` — PS §5.5, OD-PS-45), on the ally rung with the `quiet` spend where it believes it weak, and by the believed label across a broken rung — the crown's riders a foreign unit in a rebel's land (§10.3 (d), (g); PS's half); a BODY is never a passage mover — its crossing is PR's encounter (PS1, BD's route to PS); an ENEMY's patrol on a foreign stretch opens PS's `armed_incursion` with NO PS border battle (no transit record), riders meeting riders PR's `patrol_engages` (PS §5.5); a patrol never CROSSES a border by a hidden way (PS §5.5; PR §5.5's franchise rides ways only inside its own land) |
| ST (the sites) | riders subtract inside the one suppressor slot and cap; a fort puts its halves first on its holder's round, and the keep's GARRISON is the site's own complement — its road rows stand lit or dark, a distinct feeder of the same capped suppressor (§10.5; OD-PR-41); a watchtower extends notice; a grouped detachment opens `site_cleared`; the PAY band is ST's one band law, exported settlement-keyed (§3.1) |
| BD (THE BODIES, `DESIGN_FP_ARCH_BD.md`) | a body is a traveller of the ONE encounter law (its own stage calls it; MET on a trail only where the power knows the trail — `trailWatchOf`, zero otherwise) and its second WATCHER when two bodies meet (BD's `bodies_meet`) (§8.1); the riders pursue only a `hunted` gang, an `outlawed` conclave or a beast, watch a `curbed` conclave and let the rest be (§5.5); THE HUNT predicts on the town's ONE belief record of the body, `believedBodyOf` (listed by `believedBodies`), the known ways and `BODY_HABITS`, goes cold, leaves the round unridden and shadows when outmatched (§5.5); a body's road fight is `patrol_engages` with BD's `bodyStrength` (§11.2); a known trail's junction is a round stop (§2.2); a body planning a route reads `believedWatch`, banner-blind (§8.3); WARTIME IS THE GANGS' SEASON (§9.1 (e)); PR's subtraction and BD's presence row sit in the one composer (§6.1); and — the known set read BY NAME (`knownTrailsOf`), the ways on master's hidden rung through `hiddenWayEdges`, the prediction on `believedBodyOf` (its informer rows), BD's `ROAD_COVER_BY_MIGHT` and the commons' +0.2 on the encounter's cover, the riders' gleaning roll and the `watched` mark PR's stage writes through BD's one writer, the gang's `bribe` on PR's unpaid-wages slot, a LED hunt's card BD's (`hunt_taken`, lit with `bodyEffectsEnabled`) |
| LG (logistics) | journeys and fleets read the danger ledger (by construction); the sea's watch is the port's navy (W-NAVY, PR15) |
| PG (pestilence) | riders ENFORCE a quarantine the host's verb sets, never stand for it (§9.2(f)); no patrol slows a plague — and a gate's riders reach a trail only where the town knows it and watches its junction (THE BODIES' law of the trails: a closed gate stops the road, not the hidden way, L-BD 37) |
| the war volume | `patrolDraw` on the army's seed at deploy (never the per-tick capacity); W-NAVY's navies are the sea's watch; the screen within PS9's cap; the first report lights `armsNear` |
| the map | §13's WINDOW: the host's patrol rows (presence as sent) drawn by the lens when PR lights, under the lighting precondition (§12); the map's own picture while PR is dark; H16 untouched |

## §16 THE LIFECYCLE OF THE ONE PERSISTED SHAPE, AND THE SCENARIO MATRIX

### 16.1 `spatialLedgers.patrols` (requirement 22a; a requirement-23 horizon for each map)
| Path | `detachments` | `losses` | `orders` |
|---|---|---|---|
| create | only `patrolLedger.js` from the stage (§4.1 step 4: a believed threat, a hunt, a look, an escort, a join's `rides`, the DM's `clear`) | only `patrolLedger.js` at a `cut_down` verdict (or default) | only `patrolVerbApply.js` (an approved `PATROL_ORDER`) |
| read | the stage (advance, disperse, rest), the hearings' resolvers, `watchOn` (an engaged or escorting detachment covers its stretch), the dossier, the map window (§13) | the envelope (§3.1: the count minus unrecovered losses) | the envelope (`wages`), the round (`place`, `priority`) |
| persist | inside `spatialLedgers` (zero eager first-paint bytes; the `armyTransit` precedent); ABSENT when empty; a TRACKED row in `spatialUsage.js` | the same | the same |
| regenerate | rows key on settlement ids; a regeneration that keeps ids keeps them; a row naming an id that no longer exists is dropped by the normalizer | the same | the same |
| undo | the advance ring's snapshot undo restores `spatialLedgers` whole; a DM verb's undo is its proposal's undo | the same | the same |
| import | the normalizer drops malformed rows and unknown words (closed vocabularies; finite semantics — refused with a typed receipt, never coerced) | the same; rows past the horizon dropped | the same |
| migrate | none — a new key; absent ⇒ empty (every save before PR-0 reads as no riders' acts) | the same | the same |
| dark-after-lit | `patrolsEnabled` dark ⇒ nothing reads or writes the ledger; it lies inert; relit ⇒ the first normalize drops detachments whose threat is no longer believed (no stale detachment re-applies; no climax is retro-minted — requirement 24's "nothing queues") | inert; relit ⇒ read as written (a loss is history) | inert; relit ⇒ the order applies again (the DM's overlay survives as the DM left it) |
| death | the riders' town destroyed, razed or absorbed ⇒ its rows dropped at the next normalize; an ally's town dies ⇒ its members leave the detachment | dropped with the town | dropped with the town |
| veil | a detachment of a covert power is DM-only on every surface (the map's "a covert power's patrols fail closed") | — | the DM's own |
| horizon (requirement 23) | a detachment's life (dispersal or the rest bound, §7.3) | two seasons per row, ≤ 4 rows per town | until the DM clears it |

**The `placement` map and the deployment stamp**:

| Path | `placement` (per town) | the deployment record's `patrolDraw` stamp |
|---|---|---|
| create | only `patrolLedger.js`, from the stage (§4.1 step 5): `next` for t+1, the old `next` moved to `lived`, the ring advanced | only `warDeployment.js :: evaluateWarLayer`'s ONE stamp line beside the seed (counted in PR-1's host edits; `seedDeploymentState`, 72 eff, unedited), WHENEVER the court keeps riders (`points` the strength actually drawn, 0 where there was nothing to draw) |
| read | every consumer of the week through `patrolPlacementOf` (the row whose `forTick` is its tick); the window reads `lived` | the stage (riders fielded while the army is out ≤ the stamp's `riders`); the siege's Why line |
| persist | inside `spatialLedgers` (zero eager first-paint bytes); ABSENT for a town fielding none | on the deployment record (the war ledger's own lifecycle); absent ⇒ byte-identical |
| regenerate | dropped for an id that no longer exists; the next stage rewrites it | travels with its deployment |
| undo | the advance ring's snapshot restores `spatialLedgers` whole — the placement returns with the tick it belonged to | restored with the deployment record |
| import | the normalizer drops unknown cadence words and malformed halves (closed vocabularies); a `phase` outside its word's period, or a `pendingCadence` without its `pendingSince`, is dropped fail-closed (the half then re-derives at the next stage) | `worldState.js :: normalizeDeployments` RETAINS every deployment field ("`const next = { ...record };`") and validates only the fields it has a block for (`casusReasons`, `joinLedger`: "malformed, empty, or multi-row imports disappear fail-closed"), so the stamp gets ITS OWN block in that idiom — ONE host line calling `patrols/patrolDrawStamp.js :: normalizePatrolDrawStamp` (351 eff, +1): a stamp whose `riders` is not a non-negative integer or whose `points` is not a finite non-negative number is DELETED, so a malformed import reads as no draw |
| migrate | none — a new key; absent ⇒ the stage writes it at its first run (consumers in that first tick read no placement: no riders yet stood) | none — absent ⇒ no draw |
| dark-after-lit | `patrolsEnabled` dark ⇒ unread and unwritten; relit ⇒ the first stage run rewrites `next` (no placement of the dark weeks is invented; the ring restarts) | inert; a deployment seeded while lit keeps its smaller seed — lived history |
| death | dropped with the town at the next normalize | ends with its deployment |
| veil | DM-only; a covert power's halves fail closed in the window | DM-only (the war ledger's audience) |
| horizon (requirement 23) | one week ahead and one week behind; the ring four numbers | the deployment's life |

**The fork and hunt fields** (each drop-when-absent; declared as the owner's shape, OD-PR-44 · OD-PR-45):

| Path | `placement[town].forkSince` | `detachments[id].pursuedRef` | `detachments[id].leaderRef` |
|---|---|---|---|
| create | only `patrolLedger.js`, from the stage (§4.1 step 2): the FIRST week of an unbroken run in which the town is at peace with nothing believed near and its fork reads `peaceShare > PEACE_SHARE` | only `patrolLedger.js`, when a hunt forms (§4.1 step 4, §5.5): the pursued body's ref | only `patrolLedger.js`, when a hunt mission is taken (L-BD 40's card names the leader) |
| read | §7.5's harassment input (`tick − forkSince ≥ one_season`) | the hunt's prediction and catch (§5.5), the card, the window's `detachment` | the hunt's two forks (a leader's axes), the card's name, a paragon leader's lean week (§10.5) |
| persist | on the placement row inside `spatialLedgers` | on the detachment row | on the detachment row |
| regenerate | dropped with the row for an id that no longer exists | dropped when the body no longer exists (BD's `ended` ring) — the detachment disperses | dropped when the person's ledger record no longer exists; the detachment rides on unled |
| undo | the advance ring's snapshot restores it with the row | the same | the same |
| import | the normalizer DELETES a `forkSince` that is not an integer or is later than the row's `next.forTick` (fail-closed: the hold restarts) | a ref whose kind is not `body` or whose body is unknown is deleted (the hunt ends) | a ref whose kind is not `person` or whose record is unknown is deleted |
| migrate | none — a new field; absent ⇒ the hold starts at the first stage run (no grievance in a lit world's first season) | none | none |
| dark-after-lit | `patrolsEnabled` dark ⇒ inert; relit ⇒ the first stage run RESTARTS the hold where the row's `next.forTick` is not the current tick (no dark week is counted as held) | inert; relit ⇒ the first normalize drops a hunt whose trail is cold (no stale pursuit) | inert; relit ⇒ kept with its detachment, or dropped with it |
| death | dropped with the town | the body's end ends the hunt (a receipt) | the leader's death is the DM's (no named fate): the ref is dropped, the riders ride on |
| veil | DM-only machinery; never shown ahead of its week | DM-only for a covert power's riders; the card names the body by its derived name | the card names the leader; DM-only for a covert power |
| horizon (requirement 23) | one tick value, deleted the week its run breaks | the hunt's life (the cold trail, the rest bound) | the hunt's life |

**The season-ledger field** (drop-when-absent; declared as the owner's shape, ⭐ OD-PR-50):

| Path | `placement[member].weeksCovered` — `{ seasonStartTick, weeks, swept? }` |
|---|---|
| create | only `patrolLedger.js`, from the stage (§4.1 step 5): the first week of a season in which a rider of another power stood placed on one of the member's halves (from that week's `lived` rows) writes `{ seasonStartTick: tick − (tick mod INTERVAL_WEEKS.one_season), weeks: 1 }`, REPLACING a pair of an earlier season |
| read | only `bannerCoverOf` (§2.6) — by PL's protection outcome (PL §4.7 F.4) and the sweep arm of its precedence friction (PL §4.7 F.6 (b) (i)) at the season's last tick (after PR's stage, §4.1's clock) or at the member's riders' return; a pair of another season reads 0 |
| persist | on the member's placement row inside `spatialLedgers` (the row kept for the pair alone where the member fields none — its riders at the levy) |
| regenerate | dropped with the row for an id that no longer exists |
| undo | the advance ring's snapshot restores it with the row |
| import | the normalizer DELETES a pair whose `weeks` is not an integer in 1..`one_season` or whose `seasonStartTick` is not a multiple of `one_season` or lies after the row's tick (fail-closed: that season's tally restarts) |
| migrate | none — a new field; absent ⇒ 0 weeks (a season before PR-4 lit has no cover recorded, so PL opens no occasion there either, PL F.4) |
| dark-after-lit | `patrolsEnabled` dark ⇒ inert, no week counted (no placement exists); relit in the SAME season ⇒ the lit weeks already counted stand and the dark weeks add nothing (no rider stood); relit in a LATER season ⇒ the stale pair reads 0 and the first counted week replaces it (EXECUTED `cure/D-PR-r5-probe/season-r5.out` (e)) |
| death | dropped with the town; a member that leaves the polity keeps a pair no PL row reads (it has no edge to judge) until the season turns |
| veil | DM-only machinery; the card says "the crown's riders kept the hills this season", never the count |
| horizon (requirement 23) | one season — one pair, replaced at the season's turn |

**Rows added to landed shapes (no new shape):** `patrol_report` feed entries (the rumor ledgers' lifecycle: TTL 26/13, the field-merge, the weathering); PS's crossing rows' `witnessed` (PS's L2); the `MOVEMENT_SITES` row; the registry rows (SP-9's pending ledger); PL's `riders_quartered` ROWS (PL's shape 3, a closed kind with its one resentment patch, PL's writer — never a weave incident; the riders' service is PL's own summons rows); the deployment record's conditional `patrolDraw` stamp (above); the authored event's `noLook` (above); the refused join's relationship patch (the one writer's row, `patrol_join_refused`). **Requirement 14 (the edit story):** `PATROL_ORDER` is the DM's verb; detachments and losses are ENGINE-ONLY BY RECORDED DECISION — each is the outcome of a hearing the DM could decide or of the DM's own order, and the DM's correction is the hearing, the order or the undo.

**`noLook` — a field on a LANDED record, the authored event's payload (⭐ OD-PR-53)**: CREATE — only the DM, at authoring (the event composer's form; the edits-delta idiom), `true` or absent; READ — only the look's trigger (§9.2 (a)), at the tick the event drains; PERSIST — inside the event entry, on `worldState.pendingEvents` until the drain and on the member save's `campaignState.eventLog` after it (drop-when-absent; absent ⇒ investigable); REGENERATE — the event log is the save's own history and survives as the log does; UNDO — with the edit (the composer's undo before the drain; the advance's undo after it); IMPORT — any value but `true` is dropped (absent ⇒ investigable: fail-closed toward the DEFAULT the owner's word sets, a look); MIGRATE — none (absent on every landed event ⇒ every one investigable, as ruled); DARK-AFTER-LIT — `patrolsEnabled` dark: inert, no look; relit, a past event never retro-triggers a look (the look reads THIS tick's drained entries only); DEATH — dies with its event entry; VEIL — DM-authored and DM-read; never on a DM-facing surface but the composer and the look's card.

| R51 | THE LEND AT A STRONG HOLD — every tier × band at hold 0.45, 0.70 and 1.0, a direct vassal and a sub-vassal | every member with two or more riders on the round lends at least one (`max(1, ⌊n × s × hold01⌋)`), a one-rider hamlet none, no lender below `NET_HOME_MIN`, a sub-vassal at its own band share toward its liege (never the crown's), the root never past `n(root) − NET_HOME_MIN`; ten town vassals at `strong`, hold 0.60, lend 10 (EXECUTED `cure/D-PR-r5-probe/net-r5.out`) | PR-4 |
| R52 | THE RIDERS' FRANCHISE — Kell's warden leads a `modest` detachment after the Tolle gang onto the drover's way; a `capable` detachment; a round; a pursuit past the way's known `upTo`; the way crossing into Tolle's land | the warden-led `modest` detachment rides the way at `gradeLegMultiplier('hidden')` 2 (admitted by PR's own leaf read of `knownTrailsOf` — never a traveller token, §5.5); the `capable` one, the round and the pursuit past `upTo` keep to the road and the junctions (PR's leaf admits no such hop, and no kind is ever passed to `mayUseHiddenPaths`, which fails closed); no patrol crosses into Tolle's land by the way (its crossing is the road stretch's, PS §5.5) | PR-2 |
| R53 | ONE HUNT, ONE DEED — (a) Kell's unled riders drive the named Hollow Road gang off the hill road; (b) the gang cuts a lone rider down, led by a paragon (`leads`); (c) a led hunt beats the gang at the mission's close | (a) ONE `patrol_clash`, doer KELL (a tale; no court fame); (b) ONE `patrol_clash`, doer the GANG (`g:<hollow>`), `ledBy` the paragon, never also a `body_act`; (c) ONE deed, CH's `hunt_won` (doer the leader), and NO `patrol_clash` for any clash inside the hunt | PR-2 |
| R54 | THE PERSON ON A WAY — a hostile paragon's leg takes the drover's way past Kell's land; a covert envoy routed on its court's known ways | met ONLY where Kell knows the way and its eyes stand on a junction half that week (`trailWatchOf`), never by a stretch rider elsewhere; noticed with the way's `cover01` added to the person's own cover; turned back with CH's `refused` (the paragon) or caught through ES's `spy_at_target` at WHERE `road (a, b)` with the way named (the envoy) | PR-3 |
| R55 | GLEANING IN PURSUIT — Kell's riders chase the Tolle gang, which flees by a way Kell does not know, three weeks in winter snow | ONE roll a pursuit week on `bodies:glean:<unitKey>:<trailKey>:<tick>`; each success writes `by_pursuit` with `upTo` one node further through BD's writer ("as far as the ford"), the snow and the gang's haste raising the odds, a warden leader more so; a town that bought the way from a broker rolls nothing; the knowledge then fades on HB's curve (BD §2.3) | PR-2 |
| R56 | THE SWEEP ONLY ON THE LORD'S OWN NEED — Merewick the crown `staunch`; Kell a sworn member whose riders are home with riders to spare, Ashford a fellow member; (a) Merewick's own riders ride Kell's halves unasked against a robber band on Kell's round, Merewick at peace; (b) Ashford's riders ride Kell's halves by right of the banner; (c) Merewick's riders cover a stronger band beyond Kell's envelope (Kell `fielded = max`); (d) Merewick at war, its riders on Kell's roads on its war route; (e) Kell asked (`patrol_joins`) | (a) `swept` counts each such week and PL writes ONE `riders_quartered` row with its patch at the season's end; (b)–(e) `swept` 0 and NO row, while `weeks` counts (b)–(d) as the net's cover; dark `patrolsEnabled`, no tally | PR-4 |
| R57 | TWO PRODUCERS, ONE SIGNAL (PS's P38) — Kell's riders report Merewick's host marching on Kell, with no passage crossing live anywhere; then THE PASSAGE's producer stands on Kell the same week | the report alone lights `armsNear` on Kell (THE PASSAGE dark or lit); with both, Kell's threat term equals the report-only term (`THREAT_ARMS` once); with neither, exactly 0 | PR-3 / PS-3 |
| R58 | ONE LATENT TABLE — a `capable` and a `modest` rider form a detachment against a believed `strong` gang | the detachment's sum reads MN's `MIGHT_LATENT` (51 + 34 = 85 → `formidable` by `STRENGTH_BANDS`' floors) and `patrol_engages` compares it with the body's `bodyStrength` on the SAME table; no PR table exists to drift | PR-2 |
| R59 | THE REFUSED JOIN — Ashford asks Kell's riders to join against a gang; Kell refuses | ONE state-only relationship outcome on Ashford ↔ Kell through `applyRelationshipPatch`, cause `patrol_join_refused`, resentment by the DRAFT step; nothing else written | PR-4 |
| R60 | THE LOOK SWITCHED OFF — the DM authors a disappearance in Kell's land with `noLook: true`; a second without it | the first drains to Kell's `eventLog` with its `noLook` and no rider looks; the second draws one rider `looking` for one week; an import carrying `noLook: 'yes'` drops the field (investigable) | PR-2 |

## §18 WHAT A DM READS (deeds only, never forecasts; no numerals; the surface shows the outcome, the WHY waits on scrutiny)

1. "Kell keeps riders on the north road and the ford road every week." *(the dossier's defence page; scrutiny: the four gates — "a town; people enough for four patrols; its purse meagre; its watch firm")*
2. "The hill road is dangerous, but Kell's riders are on it this week." *(the fast half — the trade receipt or the believed-watch card a merchant reads)*
3. "The caravan reached Kell untouched; Kell's riders were on the hill road." *(a goods receipt)*
4. "Kell's riders confiscated Varn cloth run past the embargo." *(surface; scrutiny: the stance, Kell's gate, the riders' pay — an embargo gate's landed branch is `confiscation`, a hostile gate's `seizure`)*
5. "Kell's riders went up to the hills and found nothing but old fires." / "Kell's riders report a den at the old fort." *(a look; the second carries the zero-click suggestion "— mark it?")*
6. "Kell's riders rode out together against the band at the old fort." *(a detachment; the clearing card follows)*
7. "Kell's riders came in from the north road with word of Merewick's host at the ford." *(the war's first sign in Kell — pushed as the war's cause)*
8. "Kell's riders fell at the ford; the levy had its week." *(the screen, `cut_down`)*
9. "With the watch gone to the war, the hill road has gone to the brigands." — and where Kell holds a keep on that road: "With the watch gone to the war, the hill road beyond the keep's reach has gone to the brigands." *(the riders Kell kept rode the army's road; the hill road was beyond them; a held keep's own garrison still holds its stretches, so the sentence names only the road it no longer holds — scrutiny: the kept count, the counter term's fall, the keep's rows, the dens' rise)*
10. "Ashford's riders took a Kell spy on the river road." · "Ashford's riders turned back Kell's envoy on the hill road." *(catches, by their owners' words)*
11. "Tolle's riders rode with Ashford's against the band in the hills." *(`patrol_joins` → `rides`)*
12. "The crown's riders keep the Tolle hills since Kell's riders went to the war." · "No rider of the crown has come to the Tolle hills since Kell's riders went to the war." *(THE BANNER's net covering — or failing — a member whose riders went to the levy: PL §4.7 F.7's two lines; scrutiny: the stack as the chain, the hold, the need; the deed PL records)*
13. "Kell's envoy took the hill road; Kell did not know Ashford's riders were on it." *(planning on belief; scrutiny: what Kell believed)*
14. *(an unwatched stretch writes NOTHING a DM reads about the holder; on scrutiny of the round the DM sees "the east road is watched each season")*
15. "Merewick's riders were turned back at the Kell ford, where they rode freely last season." *(the first sign of a broken rung — §10.3 (g); scrutiny: the break, the believed label)*
16. "Kell's riders caught the Tolle gang on the drovers' path; the watch knows the way now." · "Kell's riders lost the Tolle gang in the forest and came home." *(THE HUNT — a catch on a way; a cold trail; scrutiny: the prediction's three reasons; the line never says `trail`, the map's RUNG word on the same surface)*
17. "Kell's warden knows the drover's way; the hill gang has moved its den across the border." *(the warden's ways — deterrence by belief; scrutiny: the office, the known set)*
18. "Captain Aldric led Kell's riders after the Hollow Road gang and drove it from the hills." *(a hunt led — L-BD 40; the leader named, never harmed by the engine)*
19. "The Hollow Road gang has learned Kell's riders come on the fourth week." *(scrutiny only — the learnable clock, L-BD 39; never pushed)*
20. "Kell's riders followed the Tolle gang as far as the ford; the watch knows the way that far now." *(GLEANING IN PURSUIT — L-BD 11e; scrutiny: the snow, the gang's haste, the warden leading)*
21. "Kell took it ill that the crown called Ashford's riders past it." *(THE RIDE PAST — PL's precedence friction under `unbroken`, §10.3 (b); scrutiny: the summons, Kell's answer, the row)*

## §19 OPEN QUESTIONS (none blocking; each vetoable; each also in `cure/D-PR-r5.md` §6)

1. **The tally counts the whole season** (§2.7, §10.3 (f)): a net rider placed on another need in the weeks BEFORE a member's men went to the levy counts toward that season's `protection_kept`; PL's late-answer clause covers only an occasion opened in the season's last month. The alternative: the pair also carries the first covered tick and PL counts only weeks after the answer (a second sub-field).
2. **The root's home minimum** (a refinement, §10.3 (c)): the crown keeps `NET_HOME_MIN` of its own riders on its round like any member; the alternative lets `hold01` 1.0 lend them all.
3. **The hunt among a body's friends** (a refinement, §7.5): BD §7.6 says a hunt where the commons support the body costs the court the commons' grievance through PR's `W_HARASS`; PR carries it as a second arm of its ONE input (the greater of the two, never their sum). The alternative: BD strikes the clause and such a hunt costs nothing.
4. PL's own question (`cure/D-PL-r5.md` §7, not PR's to answer): F.6 (c)'s peacetime lend is priced only as `riders_lent` WEAR, and the weave is lit nowhere; with the lend's floor (§10.3 (c)) the lend now fires. PR's composition is unchanged by either answer.

Answered and folded: the riders on the ways (~~⭐~~ OD-PR-51 as re-ruled — no token; the holder's knowledge read, §5.5) and the sweep's need (§7.6 — the crown's riders on a lord's halves are friction only for a need its own riders could have met, `swept`, ⭐ OD-PR-50). The earlier question lists, their answers and the routes to sibling volumes are in the cure record (Part 3).
